```
2. **Recipe data**: src/data/vanilla-recipes.json (count, schema: create/createStack/tile/items/groups) + the recipe engine API (src/... vanillaRecipes.ts / crafting code) — can we query "recipes producing X" and "recipes consuming X"? Note any existing reverse-lookup helpers.
3. **Item metadata available**: item names (idNames.generated.ts zh names), item stats (vanillaItemStats? data/items.ts), tooltip lines if any, rare table (vanilla-itemrare.json), UseSound etc. Anything with descriptions/lore?

6. **DebugSummonPanel** (src/core/DebugSummonPanel.ts) as a recent DOM panel precedent: search box + list pattern.
7. Any existing "guide" strings in l10n or a help panel.
```

## 📎 Attachment · skill_listing · 2026-08-13T06:33:08.166Z

```
- dataviz: Use this skill whenever you are about to create ANY chart, graph, plot, dashboard, or data visualization, in ANY output medium — an HTML or React artifact, inline SVG, plotting code in any library (matplotlib, plotly, d3, Recharts, …), an image/PNG you will render and upload, or a chart shared into Slack. Read it BEFORE writing the first line of chart code, choosing chart colors, building a stat tile / meter / KPI row, or laying out a dashboard. Produces visualizations that read as one system — elegant, accessible, consistent in light and dark — using a brand-neutral placeholder palette you swap for your own. Teaches a design-system-agnostic method: a form heuristic, a color formula with a runnable validator, mark specs, and interaction rules. A validated default palette is documented in `references/palette.md` — swap that file's values for your brand's. Triggers on: "chart", "graph", "plot", "data viz", "visualization", "dashboard", "analytics", "visualize data", "categorical colors", "sequential / diverging palette", "stat tile", "sparkline", "heatmap", "legend", "axis", "tooltip", "chart colors", "color by series".
- update-config: Use this skill to configure the Claude Code harness via settings.json. Automated behaviors ("from now on when X", "each time X", "whenever X", "before/after X") require hooks configured in settings.json - the harness executes these, not Claude, so memory/preferences cannot fulfill them. Also use for: permissions ("allow X", "add permission", "move permission to"), env vars ("set X=Y"), hook troubleshooting, or any changes to settings.json/settings.local.json files. Examples: "allow npm commands", "add bq permission to global settings", "move permission to user settings", "set DEBUG=true", "when claude stops show X". For simple settings like theme/model, suggest the /config command.
- keybindings-help: Use when the user wants to customize keyboard shortcuts, rebind keys, add chord bindings, or modify ~/.claude/keybindings.json. Examples: "rebind ctrl+s", "add a chord shortcut", "change the submit key", "customize keybindings".
- simplify: Review the changed code for reuse, simplification, efficiency, and altitude cleanups, then apply the fixes. Quality only — it does not hunt for bugs; use /code-review for that.
- fewer-permission-prompts: Scan your transcripts for common read-only Bash and MCP tool calls, then add a prioritized allowlist to project .claude/settings.json to reduce permission prompts.
- loop: Run a prompt or slash command on a recurring interval (e.g. /loop 5m /foo, defaults to 10m) - When the user wants to set up a recurring task, poll for status, or run something repeatedly on an interval (e.g. "check the deploy every 5 minutes", "keep running /babysit-prs"). Do NOT invoke for one-off tasks.
- claude-api: Reference for the Claude API / Anthropic SDK — model ids, pricing, params, streaming, tool use, MCP, agents, caching, token counting, model migration.
TRIGGER — read BEFORE opening the target file; don't skip because it "looks like a one-liner" — whenever: the prompt names Claude/Anthropic in any form (Claude, Anthropic, Fable, Opus, Sonnet, Haiku, `anthropic`, `@anthropic-ai`, `claude-*`, `us.anthropic.*`, `[1m]`); the user asks about an LLM (pricing/model choice/limits/caching) — never answer from memory; OR the task is LLM-shaped with provider unstated (agent/MCP/tool-definition/multi-agent/RAG/LLM-judge/computer-use; generate/summarize/extract/classify/rewrite/converse over NL; debugging refusals/cutoffs/streaming/tool-calls/tokens).
SKIP only when another provider is being worked on (overrides all triggers): OpenAI/GPT/Gemini/Llama/Mistral/Cohere/Ollama named in the query; OR `grep -rE 'openai|langchain_openai|google.generativeai|genai|mistralai|cohere|ollama'` over the project hits (run this grep FIRST if no provider named — don't Read the file).
- run: Launch and drive this project's app to see a change working. Use when asked to run, start, or screenshot the app, or to confirm a change works in the real app (not just tests). First looks for a project skill that already covers launching the app; otherwise falls back to built-in patterns per project type (CLI, server, TUI, Electron, browser-driven, library).
- init: Initialize a new CLAUDE.md file with codebase documentation
- review: Review a GitHub pull request; for your working diff use /code-review
- security-review: Complete a security review of the pending changes on the current branch
```

```
104	  /** 金小动物门：任一来源解锁后仍需全图已见至少一只金种（GoldCritterUICollectionInfoProvider） */
105	  goldGate: boolean;
106	}
107	
108	function killSource(npcId: number, quick: boolean): Source {
109	  return {
110	    t: 'kill',
111	    credit: bestiaryCreditId(npcId),
112	    quick,
113	    full: KILLS_TO_FULL[npcId] ?? BESTIARY_DEFAULT_FULL_KILLS,
114	  };
115	}
116	const sightSource = (npcId: number): Source => ({ t: 'sight', credit: bestiaryCreditId(npcId) });
117	const chatSource = (npcId: number): Source => ({ t: 'chat', credit: bestiaryCreditId(npcId) });
118	
119	/** BestiaryEntry.Enemy :37 的 boss 判定（npc.boss ∨ NPCID.Sets.ShouldBeCountedAsBossForBestiary）：
120	 *  npc.boss 取 npcStats 表（1456 SetDefaults boss=true 的 22 条：EoC/骷髅王/世吞头/蜂后/
121	 *  肉山/机械三王/世纪之花/石巨人/双月/邪教徒/史莱姆皇后/光女/火星飞碟核心/火把神/鹿角怪），
122	 *  Sets 补充 {517,422,507,493,13,664}（四柱/世吞头/火把神）。BossBestiaryInfoElement
123	 *  挂载（Filters.ByBoss）与 quickUnlock 同源 */
124	export function isBossForBestiary(npcId: number): boolean {
125	  return npcStats(npcId)[1] === 1 || COUNTED_AS_BOSS.has(npcId);
126	}
127	
128	let entryCache: BestiaryEntryDef[] | null = null;
129	
130	/** 条目注册表（BestiaryDatabaseNPCsPopulator.Populate 的数据层等价；惰性构建一次） */
131	export function bestiaryEntries(): BestiaryEntryDef[] {
132	  if (entryCache) return entryCache;
133	  const out: BestiaryEntryDef[] = [];
134	  for (const key of Object.keys(NPC_NAME_BY_ID)) {
135	    const id = Number(key);
136	    if (!Number.isInteger(id) || id === 0 || HIDE.has(id)) continue;
137	    const isTown = BESTIARY_TOWN_IDS.has(id);
138	    // AddEmptyEntries_CrittersAndEnemies_Automated：isLikeATownNPC 走手工城镇表，
139	    // 其余按 CountsAsACritter 分小动物/敌怪
140	    const kind: BestiaryEntryDef['kind'] = isTown ? 'town' : countsAsACritter(id) ? 'critter' : 'enemy';
141	    let sources: Source[];
142	    let goldGate = false;
143	    const goldNormals = GOLD_CRITTER_NORMALS[id];
144	    if (goldNormals) {
145	      // 金小动物：本体遇 + 同族普通种遇；解锁态恒 0/4
146	      sources = [sightSource(id), ...goldNormals.map(sightSource)];
147	      goldGate = true;
148	    } else if (id in CRITTER_PAIR) {
149	      sources = [sightSource(id), sightSource(CRITTER_PAIR[id])];
150	    } else if (id in MULTI_SOURCES) {
151	      sources = MULTI_SOURCES[id].map((s) =>
152	        s === 'kill' ? killSource(id, isQuick(id))
153	          : s === 'chat' ? chatSource(id)
154	            : s[1] === 'kill'
155	              ? killSource(s[0], s[2] ?? isQuick(s[0]))
156	              : chatSource(s[0]));
157	    } else if (id in KILL_CREDIT_FROM) {
158	      // 击杀记账归并族（Populator :600-616）：读母体杀数，quickUnlock=true
159	      sources = [killSource(KILL_CREDIT_FROM[id], true)];
160	    } else if (kind === 'town') {
161	      sources = [chatSource(id)];
162	    } else if (kind === 'critter') {
163	      sources = [sightSource(id)];
164	    } else {
165	      sources = [killSource(id, isQuick(id))];
166	    }
167	    out.push({ creditId: bestiaryCreditId(id), kind, sources, goldGate });
168	  }
169	  entryCache = out;
170	  return out;
171	}
172	
173	function isQuick(id: number): boolean {
174	  return isBossForBestiary(id) || QUICK_UNLOCK_IDS.has(id);
175	}
176	
177	/** CommonEnemyUICollectionInfoProvider.GetUnlockStateByKillCount（:60-85） */
178	export function unlockStateByKillCount(killCount: number, quick: boolean, full: number): UnlockState {
179	  const half = Math.floor(full / 2);
180	  const fifth = Math.floor(full / 5);
181	  if (quick && killCount > 0) return UnlockState.DropsWithRates;
182	  if (killCount >= full) return UnlockState.DropsWithRates;
183	  if (killCount >= half) return UnlockState.DropsNoRates;
184	  if (killCount >= fifth) return UnlockState.Stats;
185	  if (killCount >= 1) return UnlockState.PortraitOnly;
186	  return UnlockState.NotKnown;
187	}
188	
189	export interface BestiarySave {
190	  /** 杀数（creditId → 次数） */
191	  k: Record<string, number>;
192	  /** 遇数（creditId 集） */
193	  s: string[];
194	  /** 聊天解锁（creditId 集） */
195	  c: string[];
196	}
197	
198	/**
199	 * 图鉴统计器（BestiaryUnlocksTracker 1:1）——原版是随世界文件持久化的
200	 * IPersistentPerWorldContent（WorldFile.cs:3399/3405），挂在 World 上。
201	 */
202	export class BestiaryTracker {
203	  readonly kills = new Map<string, number>();
204	  readonly sights = new Set<string>();
205	  readonly chats = new Set<string>();
206	
207	  /** NPCKillsTracker.RegisterKill（杀数上限 999999999） */
208	  registerKill(netId: number): void {
209	    const id = bestiaryCreditId(netId);
210	    const next = Math.min((this.kills.get(id) ?? 0) + 1, KILL_CAP);
211	    this.kills.set(id, next);
212	  }
213	
214	  /** NPCWasNearPlayerTracker.RegisterWasNearby（首遇置位即可，幂等） */
215	  registerSight(netId: number): void {
216	    this.sights.add(bestiaryCreditId(netId));
217	  }
218	
219	  /** NPCWasChatWithTracker.RegisterChatStartWith（幂等） */
220	  registerChat(netId: number): void {
221	    this.chats.add(bestiaryCreditId(netId));
222	  }
223	
224	  private anyGoldSeen(): boolean {
225	    for (const g of GOLD_CRITTER_IDS) {
226	      if (this.sights.has(bestiaryCreditId(g))) return true;
227	    }
228	    return false;
229	  }
230	
231	  /** 条目解锁档（各 UIInfoProvider.GetEntryUICollectionInfo 的数据层等价） */
232	  unlockState(entry: BestiaryEntryDef): UnlockState {
233	    let state = UnlockState.NotKnown;
234	    for (const s of entry.sources) {
235	      const st = s.t === 'kill'
236	        ? unlockStateByKillCount(this.kills.get(s.credit) ?? 0, s.quick, s.full)
237	        : (s.t === 'sight' ? this.sights.has(s.credit) : this.chats.has(s.credit))
238	          ? UnlockState.DropsWithRates
239	          : UnlockState.NotKnown;
240	      if (st > state) state = st;
241	    }
242	    if (entry.goldGate && state > UnlockState.NotKnown && !this.anyGoldSeen()) {
243	      // GoldCritterUICollectionInfoProvider.TryFindingOneGoldCritterThatIsAlreadyUnlocked
244	      return UnlockState.NotKnown;
245	    }
246	    return state;
247	  }
248	
249	  /** 条目是否已解锁（完成度分母口径：UnlockState > NotKnown） */
250	  isUnlocked(entry: BestiaryEntryDef): boolean {
251	    return this.unlockState(entry) > UnlockState.NotKnown;
252	  }
253	
254	  /** Main.GetBestiaryProgressReport（:65003-65020）——percent=已解锁/总数，总数 0 → 1 */
255	  completion(): { total: number; unlocked: number; percent: number } {
256	    const entries = bestiaryEntries();
257	    let unlocked = 0;
258	    for (const e of entries) if (this.isUnlocked(e)) unlocked++;
259	    return {
260	      total: entries.length,
261	      unlocked,
262	      percent: entries.length === 0 ? 1 : unlocked / entries.length,
263	    };
264	  }
265	
266	  toSave(): BestiarySave {
267	    const k: Record<string, number> = {};
268	    for (const [id, n] of this.kills) k[id] = n;
269	    return { k, s: [...this.sights], c: [...this.chats] };
270	  }
271	
272	  static fromSave(data: BestiarySave | undefined): BestiaryTracker {
273	    const t = new BestiaryTracker();
274	    if (!data) return t;
275	    if (data.k) for (const [id, n] of Object.entries(data.k)) {
276	      if (typeof n === 'number' && n > 0) t.kills.set(id, Math.min(n, KILL_CAP));
277	    }
278	    if (data.s) for (const id of data.s) t.sights.add(id);
279	    if (data.c) for (const id of data.c) t.chats.add(id);
280	    return t;
281	  }
282	
283	  /** WorldGen.cs:7068 Reset（世界重生成时清零） */
284	  reset(): void {
285	    this.kills.clear();
286	    this.sights.clear();
287	    this.chats.clear();
288	  }
289	}
290	
291	/** NPC.cs:79701 IsNPCValidForBestiaryKillCredit（121 不计；EoW 三段需 boss 实例旗） */
292	export function isNpcValidForBestiaryKillCredit(vanillaId: number, isBoss: boolean): boolean {
293	  if (vanillaId === 121) return false;
294	  if (vanillaId === 13 || vanillaId === 14 || vanillaId === 15) return isBoss;
295	  return true;
296	}
297	
298	// ============ 排序 id + 稀有度星（ContentSamples.NpcBestiarySortingId / NpcBestiaryRarityStars） ============
299	// 数据来源 tools/extract-bestiary.mjs → vanilla-bestiary.json：
300	//   TownNPCBestiaryPriority :4427 / NormalGoldCritterBestiaryPriority :4407 /
301	//   BossBestiaryPriority :4416（GetSortedBestiaryEntriesList orderby 三键）+
302	//   npcStats（1456 SetDefaults type 链逐 id 求值的 rarity/boss/damage/defense/lifeMax/aiStyle
303	//   六元组——FillNpcRarities :1072-1083 的公式入参，负 id 全在 Hide 表不进图鉴故未提取）+
304	//   ContentSamples.FillNpcRarities 显式覆盖(:1084-1220)
305	// 数据来源 tools/extract-bestiary-spawn.mjs → vanilla-bestiary-spawn.json：
306	//   byNpc + commonFilters（BestiaryHelper.GetLowestBiomeGroupIndex 的组序表）
307	import bestiaryJson from './vanilla-bestiary.json';
308	import spawnJson from './vanilla-bestiary-spawn.json';
309	
310	type BestiaryJson = typeof bestiaryJson;
311	type SpawnJson = typeof spawnJson;
312	const TOWN_PRIORITY: number[] = (bestiaryJson as BestiaryJson).townNpcBestiaryPriority;
313	const GOLD_CRITTER_PRIORITY: number[] = (bestiaryJson as BestiaryJson).normalGoldCritterBestiaryPriority;
314	const BOSS_PRIORITY: number[] = (bestiaryJson as BestiaryJson).bossBestiaryPriority;
315	const STAR_OVERRIDES: Record<string, number> = (bestiaryJson as BestiaryJson).rarityStarOverrides;
316	/** npcStats 元组序（extract-bestiary.mjs）：[rarity, boss, damage, defense, lifeMax, aiStyle] */
317	type NpcStatsTuple = [number, number, number, number, number, number];
318	const NPC_STATS = (bestiaryJson as BestiaryJson).npcStats as unknown as Record<string, NpcStatsTuple>;
319	const COMMON_FILTERS: string[] = (spawnJson as unknown as SpawnJson).commonFilters;
320	const SPAWN_BY_NPC: Record<string, string[]> = (spawnJson as unknown as SpawnJson).byNpc;
321	
322	/** 逐 NPC 投影（ContentSamples.NpcsByNetId 的 rarity/boss/战力/aiStyle 读数） */
323	function npcStats(npcId: number): NpcStatsTuple {
324	  return NPC_STATS[String(npcId)] ?? [0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0];
325	}
326	
327	/** NPC.isLikeATownNPC（NPC.cs:6772-6781）：453 恒真，其余走 townNPC 旗 */
328	export function isLikeATownNpc(npcId: number): boolean {
329	  return npcId === 453 || BESTIARY_TOWN_IDS.has(npcId);
330	}
331	
332	/** ContentSamples.GetNPCBestiaryRarityStarsCount（:1223-1285）1:1：
333	 *  1 + rarity（1→+1 / 2→+1.5 / 3→+2 / 4→+2.5 / 5→+3 / >5→+3.5）+ boss 0.5 +
334	 *  战力阈（>50→+1 / >150→+1.5 / >500→+2 / >1000→+2.5 / >5000→+3 / >10000→+3.5），
335	 *  钳 5 取整（C# (int) 截断）。FillNpcRarities 先全量算公式再显式覆盖
336	 *  （城镇 1-3 / Boss 4-5 段），覆盖优先。入参全部取 npcStats 表（1456 SetDefaults
337	 *  求值）——Classic 难度 ScaleStats 乘数恒 1，链上原值即公式读数 */
338	export function bestiaryRarityStars(npcId: number): number {
339	  const o = STAR_OVERRIDES[npcId];
340	  if (o !== undefined) return o;
341	  const [rarity, boss, damage, defense, lifeMax] = npcStats(npcId);
342	  let n = 1 + rarity;
343	  if (rarity === 1) n += 1;
344	  else if (rarity === 2) n += 1.5;
345	  else if (rarity === 3) n += 2;
346	  else if (rarity === 4) n += 2.5;
347	  else if (rarity === 5) n += 3;
348	  else if (rarity > 0) n += 3.5;
349	  if (boss) n += 0.5;
350	  const power = damage + defense + Math.floor(lifeMax / 4);   // npc.lifeMax / 4 整除
351	  if (power > 10000) n += 3.5;
352	  else if (power > 5000) n += 3;
353	  else if (power > 1000) n += 2.5;
354	  else if (power > 500) n += 2;
355	  else if (power > 150) n += 1.5;
356	  else if (power > 50) n += 1;
357	  return Math.trunc(Math.min(n, 5));
358	}
359	
360	/** BestiaryHelper.GetLowestBiomeGroupIndex（ContentSamples.cs:755-766）：
361	 *  commonElements（GetCommonInfoElementsForFilters :239-301，57 条）自尾向头
362	 *  找第一个出现在条目 Info 中的元素 → 返回该组号（即命中的最大组号）；
363	 *  一个公共元素都不含 = int.MaxValue */
364	export function lowestBiomeGroupIndex(npcId: number): number {
365	  const have = SPAWN_BY_NPC[String(npcId)];
366	  if (!have) return Number.MAX_SAFE_INTEGER;
367	  for (let i = COMMON_FILTERS.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
368	    if (have.includes(COMMON_FILTERS[i])) return i;
369	  }
370	  return Number.MAX_SAFE_INTEGER;
371	}
372	
373	let sortingIdCache: Map<number, number> | null = null;
374	
375	/** ContentSamples.CreateBestiarySortingIds（:1063-1073）——GetSortedBestiaryEntriesList
376	 *  （BestiaryHelper，ContentSamples.cs:745-753）九键 orderby 链的稳定全序 → 1..N 序号：
377	 *  城镇优先位 → !isLikeATownNPC → 金小动物优先位 → !CountsAsACritter → Boss 优先位 →
378	 *  群系组序（GetLowestBiomeGroupIndex）→ aiStyle → 战力 → 星数（全升序；
379	 *  IndexOf 缺席 = MAX，Boss 缺席 = -1）。原版 NpcsByNetId 全集（-65..696，含 Hide）
380	 *  参与 orderby、排序后才 RemoveAll(Hide)——序号只发给非 Hide 条目，故编号跳过 Hide。 */
381	export function bestiarySortingId(npcId: number): number {
382	  if (!sortingIdCache) {
383	    sortingIdCache = new Map();
384	    const townIdx = new Map(TOWN_PRIORITY.map((id, i) => [id, i]));
385	    const goldIdx = new Map(GOLD_CRITTER_PRIORITY.map((id, i) => [id, i]));
386	    const bossIdx = new Map(BOSS_PRIORITY.map((id, i) => [id, i]));
387	    const MAX = Number.MAX_SAFE_INTEGER;
388	    const sortKey = (id: number): number[] => {
389	      const [, , damage, defense, lifeMax, aiStyle] = npcStats(id);
390	      return [
391	        townIdx.get(id) ?? MAX,                 // GetBestiaryTownPriority
392	        isLikeATownNpc(id) ? 0 : 1,             // !isLikeATownNPC
393	        goldIdx.get(id) ?? MAX,                 // GetBestiaryNormalGoldCritterPriority
394	        countsAsACritter(id) ? 0 : 1,           // !CountsAsACritter
395	        bossIdx.get(id) ?? -1,                  // GetBestiaryBossPriority
396	        lowestBiomeGroupIndex(id),              // GetLowestBiomeGroupIndex（公共过滤元素组号）
397	        aiStyle,
398	        damage + defense + Math.floor(lifeMax / 4), // GetBestiaryPowerLevel（整除）
399	        bestiaryRarityStars(id),
400	      ];
401	    };
402	    const ids = Object.keys(NPC_NAME_BY_ID)
403	      .map(Number)
404	      .filter((n) => Number.isInteger(n) && n !== 0);
405	    ids.sort((a, b) => {
406	      const ka = sortKey(a);
407	      const kb = sortKey(b);
408	      for (let i = 0; i < ka.length; i++) {
409	        if (ka[i] !== kb[i]) return ka[i] - kb[i];
410	      }
411	      return a - b;   // 全键平手：稳定序（原版 List 稳定排序保持注册序）
412	    });
413	    let rank = 0;   // 序号只发给非 Hide 条目（CreateBestiarySortingIds 遍历 RemoveAll 后的表）
414	    for (const id of ids) {
415	      if (HIDE.has(id)) continue;
416	      sortingIdCache.set(id, ++rank);
417	    }
418	  }
419	  return sortingIdCache.get(npcId) ?? Number.MAX_SAFE_INTEGER;
420	}
421	

```

```
37	/** 纯过滤（单测对象）：数字/名字子串，命中 id 优先排前 */
38	export function filterSummonEntries(q: string, limit = 240): SummonEntry[] {
39	  const s = q.trim().toLowerCase();
40	  if (!s) return SUMMON_ENTRIES.slice(0, limit);
41	  const hit: SummonEntry[] = [];
42	  for (const e of SUMMON_ENTRIES) {
43	    if (String(e.id) === s || e.name.toLowerCase().includes(s)) hit.push(e);
44	    if (hit.length >= limit * 2) break;

46	  // 完全等于 id 的排最前

133	  /** 事件触发行（hooks.events 有条目才显示） */
134	  private renderEvents(): void {
135	    const box = this.el?.querySelector<HTMLElement>('[data-role=events]');
136	    if (!box) return;
137	    const events = this.hooks.events ?? [];
138	    if (!events.length || !this.hooks.triggerEvent) return;
139	    box.style.display = 'flex';
140	    for (const ev of events) {
141	      const btn = document.createElement('button');
142	      btn.textContent = ev.label;
143	      btn.style.cssText = 'background:#182838;color:#9CD;border:1px solid #356;border-radius:4px;cursor:pointer;padding:2px 8px;font:inherit;';
144	      btn.addEventListener('click', () => {

148	      });
149	      box.appendChild(btn);
150	    }
151	  }
152	
153	  close(): void {
154	    this.el?.remove();
155	    this.el = null;
156	    this.listEl = null;
157	  }
158	
159	  private msg(text: string, color = '#8C8'): void {
160	    const m = this.el?.querySelector<HTMLElement>('[data-role=msg]');
161	    if (m) { m.textContent = text; m.style.color = color; }
162	  }
163	
164	  private render(): void {
165	    if (!this.listEl) return;
166	    const rows = filterSummonEntries(this.query);
167	    this.listEl.innerHTML = '';
168	    for (const e of rows) {
169	      const row = document.createElement('div');
170	      row.style.cssText = 'display:flex;gap:6px;padding:2px 6px;cursor:pointer;border-radius:3px;';
171	      row.addEventListener('mouseenter', () => { row.style.cssText += ROW_HOVER; });
172	      row.addEventListener('mouseleave', () => { row.style.cssText = row.style.cssText.replace(ROW_HOVER, ''); });
173	      const disp = this.hooks.nameOf(e.id) || e.name;
174	      row.innerHTML =
175	        `<span style="color:#789;min-width:34px;text-align:right;">${e.id}</span>` +
176	        `<span style="flex:1;color:${e.town ? '#8CF' : e.critter ? '#8D8' : '#EEE'};overflow:hidden;text-overflow:ellipsis;white-space:nowrap;">${disp}</span>` +
177	        (e.boss ? '<span style="color:#F66;">BOSS</span>' : '') +
178	        (e.town ? '<span style="color:#8CF;">镇</span>' : '') +
179	        (e.critter ? '<span style="color:#8D8;">宠</span>' : '');
180	      row.addEventListener('click', () => {
181	        const err = this.hooks.spawn(e.id, this.count, this.atMouse);
182	        if (err) this.msg(err, '#F88');
183	        else this.msg(`已召唤 ${disp}${this.count > 1 ? ` ×${this.count}` : ''}`);
184	      });
185	      this.listEl.appendChild(row);
186	    }
187	    if (!rows.length) this.listEl.innerHTML = '<div style="color:#889;padding:6px;">无匹配</div>';
188	  }
189	}
```

```
174	export function envOk(r: VanillaRecipe, ctx: VanillaCraftCtx): boolean {
175	  if (r.tile >= 0) {
176	    const expanded = expandStationTiles(r.tile);
177	    // 任一等价站点在附近（adjTile[requiredTile] + CountsAs 继承）
178	    const ok = expanded.some((t) => {
179	      const internal = SHEET_TO_INTERNAL.get(t);
180	      return internal !== undefined && ctx.nearbyInternal.has(internal);
181	    });
182	    if (!ok) return false;
183	  }
184	  if (r.water && !ctx.nearWater) return false;
185	  if (r.honey && !ctx.nearHoney) return false;
186	  if (r.lava && !ctx.nearLava) return false;
187	  if (r.snow && !ctx.zoneSnow) return false;
188	  if (r.graveyard && !ctx.zoneGraveyard) return false;
189	  return true;
190	}

197	    if (vid >= 0 && !m.has(vid)) m.set(vid, def.key);
198	  }

207	export function ingredientKey(vid: number): string | undefined {
208	  if (vid >= 1000000) {
209	    const items = RECIPE_GROUP_ITEMS[String(vid - 1000000)];
210	    if (items) for (const it of items) {
211	      const k = VID_TO_KEY.get(it);
212	      if (k) return k;
213	    }
214	    return undefined;
215	  }
216	  return VID_TO_KEY.get(vid);

220	export function availableRecipes(inv: Inventory, ctx: VanillaCraftCtx): Array<{ r: VanillaRecipe; count: number }> {
221	  const owned = collectOwnedItems(inv);
222	  const out: Array<{ r: VanillaRecipe; count: number }> = [];
223	  for (const r of VANILLA_RECIPES) {
224	    if (!envOk(r, ctx)) continue;
225	    const count = craftableCount(r, owned);
226	    if (count > 0) out.push({ r, count });
227	  }
228	  return out;
229	}

231	/** 执行合成（CraftingRequests.CraftLocally 主干）：扣材料→返成品 key（放置进背包由调用方） */
232	export function consumeMaterials(inv: Inventory, r: VanillaRecipe, times = 1, alchemyTable = false): boolean {
233	  // ---- 第一阶段：可用性校验（不扣）——CraftingRequests.cs:86-102 每次迭代重查、
234	  // 永不过扣。旧实现"边扣边查、不足 return false 不回滚"=材料部分销毁零产出
235	  // （shift ×10 合成时整批蒸发,2026-08-13 修）----
236	  for (const req of r.items) {
237	    let need = req.stack * times;
238	    const gid = groupOf(r, req.id);
239	    if (gid >= 0) {
240	      for (const g of (RECIPE_GROUP_ITEMS[String(gid)] ?? [])) {
241	        if (need <= 0) break;
242	        const iid = invIndexOfVanilla(g);
243	        if (iid >= 0) need -= Math.min(inv.countOf(iid), need);
244	      }
245	      if (need > 0) return false;
246	    } else {
247	      const iid = invIndexOfVanilla(req.id);
248	      if (iid < 0 || inv.countOf(iid) < need) return false;
249	    }
250	  }
251	  // ---- 第二阶段：执行扣除（校验已过,理论不触不足;保持原折扣/组扣语义）----
252	  for (const req of r.items) {
253	    let need = req.stack * times;
254	    // 炼金折扣（GetIngredientCraftingDiscount Recipe.cs:213-227：alchemy 配方且 alchemyTable
255	    // 时每个材料独立掷 1/3 概率该份免费——按"每份独立掷骰"逐个扣）
256	    if (r.alchemy && alchemyTable && times === 1) {
257	      let discounted = 0;
258	      for (let k = 0; k < need; k++) if (Math.random() < 1 / 3) discounted++;
259	      need -= discounted;
260	      if (need <= 0) continue;
261	    }
262	    // 组材料（groupOf:占位 id/哨兵 id 统一判定,ConsumeFromInventory :451 语义——
263	    // 组槽可扣组内任一成员,依序补足）
264	    const gid2 = groupOf(r, req.id);
265	    if (gid2 >= 0) {
266	      for (const g of (RECIPE_GROUP_ITEMS[String(gid2)] ?? [])) {
267	        if (need <= 0) break;
268	        const iid = invIndexOfVanilla(g);
269	        if (iid < 0) continue;
270	        const take = Math.min(inv.countOf(iid), need);
271	        if (take > 0) { inv.remove(iid, take); need -= take; }
272	      }
273	      if (need > 0) return true; // 阶段一已过,残余只可能来自折扣外并发,放行不吞
274	    } else {
275	      const iid = invIndexOfVanilla(req.id);
276	      if (iid >= 0) inv.remove(iid, Math.min(inv.countOf(iid), need));
277	    }
278	  }
279	  return true;
280	}

282	/** 原版 item id → Inventory 内部 item id（缓存） */
283	const _iidCache = new Map<number, number>();
284	export function invIndexOfVanilla(vid: number): number {
285	  let hit = _iidCache.get(vid);
286	  if (hit !== undefined) return hit;
287	  const key = VID_TO_KEY.get(vid);
288	  // ITEM_DEFS 下标即内部 id——线性扫一次后缓存
289	  for (let i = 0; i < ITEM_DEFS.length; i++) {
290	    const d = ITEM_DEFS[i];
291	    const v = d.vid ?? (d.key.startsWith('vi_') ? parseInt(d.key.slice(3), 10) : -1);
292	    if (v === vid) { hit = i; break; }
293	  }
294	  hit ??= -1;
295	  _iidCache.set(vid, hit);
296	  return hit;
297	}

300	export const MATERIAL_VIDS = (() => {
301	  const s = new Set<number>();
302	  for (const r of VANILLA_RECIPES) for (const req of r.items) s.add(req.id);
303	  return s;
304	})();
```

```
2	// UIBestiaryTest.cs（1.4.5.6）+ UI.Elements/UIBestiaryEntryGrid.cs：
3	//   网格 72×72 单元（GetEntriesToShow：cols=floor(w/72) rows=floor(h/72)，分页按页偏移）
4	//   范围文本 "{first}-{last} ({total})"（GetRangeText）；FixBestiaryRange 钳制
5	//   顶栏：◀ ▶ + 范围 + 排序 + 筛选 + 搜索（UIWrappedSearchBar）
6	//   底栏：完成度百分比 + 进度条（FillPercent；填充 rgb(51,137,255) 底 rgb(35,43,81)）
7	//   面板底色 rgb(33,43,79)*0.8（BuildPage UIPanel.BackgroundColor）
8	//   条目卡：头像（NPC 表首帧）+ 名字；未解锁剪影（UnlockableNPCEntryIcon 语义）
9	//   详情栏（右侧，UIBestiaryEntryInfoPage 位置）：
10	//     解锁档 UnlockState 1 头像/2 +属性/3 +掉落/4 +掉落率（Bestiary.ts unlockState）
11	//     掉落表 = vanilla-npcdrops.json 规则树展平（ItemDropBestiaryInfoElement 近似）
12	// 打开入口：背包面板图鉴按钮（原版 BestiaryMenuButton Main.cs:41905）+ 暂停菜单。
13	// 键位：Esc / E 关闭（DOM 面板惯例；capture 阶段拦截防 main.ts 暂停键二次消费）。
14	// 已闭合登记（数据层 → UI 消费）：
15	//   - 排序：Sort_BestiaryID（ContentSamples.NpcBestiarySortingId 九键链全键提取，
16	//     含 GetLowestBiomeGroupIndex）/ Sort_Rarity（NpcBestiaryRarityStars，npcStats 投影）
17	//   - 头像背景：IBestiaryBackgroundImagePathAndColorProvider（MapBG1-42 按出没环境
18	//     推导，偏好 AddTags/世界恶双路/月总特例；网格=首个带图条件，详情=末个+bgColor 着色）
19	// 登记缺口（后续批次）：
20	//   - Visuals.* 装饰叠层（MapBGOverlay1-9：Rain/Blizzard/Sun/Moon/Meteor 等）——
21	//     提取数据已含 derivations 推导规则，DOM 头像暂只铺底图不叠装饰层
22	//   - 筛选：稀有生物（RareSpawnBestiaryInfoElement）/ 群系 / 事件标签
23	import { Lang } from '../i18n/Lang';
24	import { NPC_NAME_BY_ID } from '../i18n/idNames.generated';
25	import { BESTIARY_CREDIT_REDIRECT } from '../data/bestiaryStatics.generated';
26	import { VANILLA_NPCS } from '../data/vanillaNpcs';
27	import { npcValueOf } from '../drops/NpcDrops';
28	import dropData from '../data/vanilla-npcdrops.json';
29	import spawnData from '../data/vanilla-bestiary-spawn.json';
30	import { UISfx } from '../vui/UISfx';
31	import type { SpriteAtlas } from '../assets/SpriteAtlas';
32	import {
33	  bestiaryEntries, bestiaryCreditId, UnlockState,
34	  bestiaryRarityStars, bestiarySortingId, isBossForBestiary,
35	  type BestiaryEntryDef, type BestiaryTracker,
36	} from '../data/Bestiary';

38	/* eslint-disable @typescript-eslint/no-explicit-any */
39	type Rule = any;
40	
41	// ============ 纯逻辑（Node 可单测；DOM 渲染只是其消费方） ============
42	
43	/** UIBestiaryEntryGrid.GetEntriesToShow：单元 72×72（UIBestiaryEntryButton 尺寸） */
44	export const BST_CELL = 72;
45	
46	export function bestiaryGridSize(w: number, h: number): { cols: number; rows: number; perPage: number } {
47	  const cols = Math.max(1, Math.floor(w / BST_CELL));
48	  const rows = Math.max(1, Math.floor(h / BST_CELL));
49	  return { cols, rows, perPage: cols * rows };
50	}
51	
52	/** UIBestiaryEntryGrid.GetRangeText："{first}-{last} ({total})"，空集 "0-0 (0)" */
53	export function bestiaryRangeText(atIndex: number, last: number, perPage: number): string {
54	  const end = Math.min(last, atIndex + perPage);
55	  const first = Math.min(atIndex + 1, end);
56	  return `${first}-${end} (${last})`;
57	}
58	
59	/** FixBestiaryRange：偏移钳制到 [0, max(0, last - perPage)] */
60	export function clampBestiaryOffset(atIndex: number, offset: number, last: number, perPage: number): number {
61	  const max = Math.max(0, last - perPage);
62	  return Math.min(Math.max(atIndex + offset, 0), max);
63	}
64	
65	/** 分页数（探针口径：546 条 / 每页条数 → 页数） */
66	export function bestiaryPageCount(total: number, perPage: number): number {
67	  if (perPage <= 0) return 0;
68	  return Math.ceil(total / perPage);
69	}
70	
71	export type BestiarySortKey = 'unlocks' | 'id' | 'bestiaryId' | 'alpha' | 'rarity' | 'attack' | 'defense' | 'coins' | 'hp';
72	/** SortingSteps 注册序（UIBestiaryEntry.SortingSteps RegisterSortSteps：Unlocks → ID →
73	 *  BestiaryID → Alphabetical → Rarity → Attack → Defense → Coins → HitPoints） */
74	export const BESTIARY_SORT_KEYS: BestiarySortKey[] = ['unlocks', 'id', 'bestiaryId', 'alpha', 'rarity', 'attack', 'defense', 'coins', 'hp'];
75	
76	export function sortLabel(key: BestiarySortKey): string {
77	  switch (key) {
78	    case 'unlocks': return Lang.text('BestiaryInfo.Sort_Unlocks');
79	    case 'id': return Lang.text('BestiaryInfo.Sort_ID');
80	    case 'bestiaryId': return Lang.text('BestiaryInfo.Sort_BestiaryID');
81	    case 'rarity': return Lang.text('BestiaryInfo.Sort_Rarity');
82	    case 'alpha': return Lang.text('BestiaryInfo.Sort_Alphabetical');
83	    case 'attack': return Lang.text('BestiaryInfo.Sort_Attack');
84	    case 'defense': return Lang.text('BestiaryInfo.Sort_Defense');
85	    case 'coins': return Lang.text('BestiaryInfo.Sort_Coins');
86	    case 'hp': return Lang.text('BestiaryInfo.Sort_HitPoints');
87	  }
88	}
89	
90	export interface BestiaryFilter {
91	  search: string;
92	  /** 条目类别（本仓自有维度，无原版键） */
93	  kind: 'all' | 'enemy' | 'town' | 'critter';
94	  /** Filters.ByBoss（BestiaryInfo.IsBoss） */
95	  boss: boolean;
96	  /** Filters.ByUnlockState（BestiaryInfo.IfUnlocked）；'no' 为反向（本仓补充） */
97	  unlocked: 'all' | 'yes' | 'no';
98	}
99	
100	export const DEFAULT_BESTIARY_FILTER: BestiaryFilter = { search: '', kind: 'all', boss: false, unlocked: 'all' };
101	
102	export interface BestiaryRow {
103	  entry: BestiaryEntryDef;
104	  /** 条目代表 NPC id（图标/属性取自它；归并族取母体） */
105	  npcId: number;
106	  state: UnlockState;
107	  name: string;
108	  /** 击杀数（kill 来源 creditId 的计数；非击杀条目 0） */
109	  kills: number;
110	}
111	
112	/** creditId → 母体 NPC id（BESTIARY_CREDIT_REDIRECT 归并族取未被重定向者，正 id 优先） */
113	let repIdCache: Map<string, number> | null = null;
114	export function bestiaryRepNpcId(creditId: string): number {
115	  if (!repIdCache) {
116	    repIdCache = new Map();
117	    const R = BESTIARY_CREDIT_REDIRECT as Record<number, number>;
118	    const put = (id: number) => {
119	      const cid = NPC_NAME_BY_ID[id] ?? String(id);
120	      if (!repIdCache!.has(cid)) repIdCache!.set(cid, id);
121	    };
122	    for (const key of Object.keys(NPC_NAME_BY_ID)) {
123	      const id = Number(key);
124	      if (!Number.isInteger(id) || id === 0) continue;
125	      if (R[id] === undefined && id > 0) put(id);   // 母体（正 id）
126	    }
127	    for (const key of Object.keys(NPC_NAME_BY_ID)) {
128	      const id = Number(key);
129	      if (!Number.isInteger(id) || id === 0) continue;
130	      if (R[id] !== undefined) put(R[id]);           // 变体族回填母体
131	    }
132	    for (const key of Object.keys(NPC_NAME_BY_ID)) {
133	      const id = Number(key);
134	      // 负 netID 变体（史莱姆配色 -1..-10 / 世吞段 -11..-13）代表自身
135	      if (Number.isInteger(id) && id !== 0) put(id);
136	    }
137	  }
138	  const fallback = Number(creditId);
139	  return repIdCache.get(creditId) ?? (Number.isFinite(fallback) ? fallback : 0);
140	}
141	
142	/** BossBestiaryInfoElement 挂载集（BestiaryEntry.Enemy :37：npc.boss ∨
143	 *  ShouldBeCountedAsBossForBestiary → Filters.ByBoss）的 creditId 化 */
144	const BOSS_CREDITS = new Set(
145	  Object.keys(NPC_NAME_BY_ID)
146	    .map(Number)
147	    .filter((id) => Number.isInteger(id) && id !== 0 && isBossForBestiary(id))
148	    .map(bestiaryCreditId),
149	);
150	
151	export function isBossCredit(creditId: string): boolean {
152	  return BOSS_CREDITS.has(creditId);
153	}
154	
155	/** 全条目行化（bestiaryEntries × tracker.unlockState × 名字解析） */
156	export function bestiaryRows(tracker: BestiaryTracker): BestiaryRow[] {
157	  const out: BestiaryRow[] = [];
158	  for (const entry of bestiaryEntries()) {
159	    let kills = 0;
160	    for (const s of entry.sources) {
161	      if (s.t === 'kill') kills = Math.max(kills, tracker.kills.get(s.credit) ?? 0);
162	    }
163	    const npcId = bestiaryRepNpcId(entry.creditId);
164	    const state = tracker.unlockState(entry);
165	    out.push({
166	      entry, npcId, kills, state,
167	      // 未解锁不显示名（原版图标 hover "???"）
168	      name: state > UnlockState.NotKnown ? npcDisplayName(npcId, entry.creditId) : '???',
169	    });
170	  }
171	  return out;
172	}
173	
174	/** 条目显示名：正 id 走 Lang.npcName；负 netID（史莱姆/世吞段变体）Lang.npcName
175	 *  会取 abs 误命中他者，改按内部名直查；都缺回退 creditId */
176	export function npcDisplayName(npcId: number, fallback: string): string {
177	  if (npcId > 0) return Lang.npcName(npcId) ?? fallback;
178	  const internal = NPC_NAME_BY_ID[npcId];
179	  if (internal && Lang.has(`NPCName.${internal}`)) return Lang.text(`NPCName.${internal}`);
180	  return fallback;
181	}
182	
183	/** 排序（SortingSteps 各 Compare 的数据层等价；无数据步缺省，平手按 npcId 升序） */
184	export function sortBestiaryRows(rows: BestiaryRow[], key: BestiarySortKey): void {
185	  const stat = (r: BestiaryRow, f: 'lifeMax' | 'damage' | 'defense'): number =>
186	    VANILLA_NPCS[String(r.npcId)]?.[f] ?? -1;
187	  const cmp: Record<BestiarySortKey, (a: BestiaryRow, b: BestiaryRow) => number> = {
188	    // ByUnlockState：解锁档降序（已解锁在前）
189	    unlocks: (a, b) => b.state - a.state || a.npcId - b.npcId,
190	    // ByNetId 升序
191	    id: (a, b) => a.npcId - b.npcId,
192	    // ByBestiaryId：ContentSamples.NpcBestiarySortingId（九键 orderby 链序号）升序
193	    bestiaryId: (a, b) => bestiarySortingId(a.npcId) - bestiarySortingId(b.npcId),
194	    // ByRarity：NpcBestiaryRarityStars 降序
195	    rarity: (a, b) => bestiaryRarityStars(b.npcId) - bestiaryRarityStars(a.npcId) || a.npcId - b.npcId,
196	    // Alphabetical：显示名升序
197	    alpha: (a, b) => a.name.localeCompare(b.name, 'zh') || a.npcId - b.npcId,
198	    // ByStat 族：降序（无数据条目沉底）
199	    attack: (a, b) => stat(b, 'damage') - stat(a, 'damage') || a.npcId - b.npcId,
200	    defense: (a, b) => stat(b, 'defense') - stat(a, 'defense') || a.npcId - b.npcId,
201	    coins: (a, b) => npcValueOf(b.npcId) - npcValueOf(a.npcId) || a.npcId - b.npcId,
202	    hp: (a, b) => stat(b, 'lifeMax') - stat(a, 'lifeMax') || a.npcId - b.npcId,
203	  };
204	  rows.sort(cmp[key]);
205	}
206	
207	/** 筛选（Filters.BySearch/ByUnlockState/ByBoss + 本仓 kind 维度） */
208	export function filterBestiaryRows(rows: BestiaryRow[], f: BestiaryFilter): BestiaryRow[] {
209	  const q = f.search.trim().toLowerCase();
210	  return rows.filter((r) => {
211	    if (f.kind !== 'all' && r.entry.kind !== f.kind) return false;
212	    if (f.boss && !isBossCredit(r.entry.creditId)) return false;
213	    if (f.unlocked === 'yes' && r.state === UnlockState.NotKnown) return false;
214	    if (f.unlocked === 'no' && r.state > UnlockState.NotKnown) return false;
215	    // BySearch：未解锁条目不提供搜索串（原版 NamePlate 隐藏）
216	    if (q && (r.state === UnlockState.NotKnown || !r.name.toLowerCase().includes(q))) return false;
217	    return true;
218	  });
219	}
220	
221	// ============ 掉落表展平（ItemDropBestiaryInfoElement 近似） ============
222	
223	export interface DropLine {
224	  item: number;
225	  /** 掉率（0-1；null = 非概率掉落如 noRepeat 池/事件掉落） */
226	  chance: number | null;
227	  min: number;
228	  max: number;
229	  /** 条件名（Bestiary.ItemDropConditions.<name> 取文案） */
230	  cond: string | null;
231	  /** 专家/大师分支标签 */
232	  tag: string | null;
233	}
234	
235	const DROP_ENTRIES: Array<{ npcs?: number[]; global?: boolean; rule: Rule }> = (dropData as any).entries;
236	const dropRulesByNpc = new Map<number, Rule[]>();
237	for (const e of DROP_ENTRIES) {
238	  if (e.global) continue; // 全局规则不计入条目掉落表（原版按条目注册展示）
239	  for (const n of e.npcs ?? []) {
240	    let l = dropRulesByNpc.get(n);
241	    if (!l) dropRulesByNpc.set(n, l = []);
242	    l.push(e.rule);
243	  }
244	}
245	
246	function pushDrop(out: DropLine[], item: number, chance: number | null, min: number, max: number, cond: string | null, tag: string | null) {
247	  if (!item) return;
248	  out.push({ item, chance: chance === null ? null : Math.min(1, chance), min, max, cond, tag });
249	}
250	
251	function walkDrop(rule: Rule, out: DropLine[], gate: number, tag: string | null, cond: string | null): void {
252	  if (!rule || typeof rule !== 'object') return;
253	  const c = rule.cond?.name ?? cond;
254	  const num = rule.num ?? 1;
255	  switch (rule.k) {
256	    case 'common': case 'rerolls': case 'cond':
257	    case 'local': case 'perPlayer':
258	      pushDrop(out, rule.item, gate * (num / (rule.denom ?? 1)), rule.min ?? 1, rule.max ?? 1, c, tag);
259	      break;
260	    case 'oneOf':
261	      for (const id of rule.ids ?? []) pushDrop(out, id, gate * (num / (rule.denom ?? 1)), 1, 1, c, tag);
262	      break;
263	    case 'expert': // DropBasedOnExpertMode：normal/expert 双列展示
264	      walkDrop(rule.normal, out, gate, tag, cond);
265	      walkDrop(rule.expert, out, gate, 'IsExpert', cond);
266	      return;
267	    case 'master':
268	      walkDrop(rule.def, out, gate, tag, cond);
269	      walkDrop(rule.master, out, gate, 'IsMasterMode', cond);
270	      return;
271	    case 'masterExpert': // master > expert > def
272	      walkDrop(rule.def, out, gate, tag, cond);
273	      walkDrop(rule.expert, out, gate, 'IsExpert', cond);
274	      walkDrop(rule.master, out, gate, 'IsMasterMode', cond);
275	      return;
276	    case 'masterCommon':
277	      pushDrop(out, rule.item, gate, 1, 1, c, 'IsMasterMode');
278	      break;
279	    case 'masterAll':
280	      pushDrop(out, rule.item, gate / (rule.denom ?? 1), 1, 1, c, 'IsMasterMode');
281	      break;
282	    case 'bossBag': // 非专家分支 = DropNothing
283	      pushDrop(out, rule.item, gate, 1, 1, c, 'IsExpert');
284	      break;
285	    case 'oneOfRules': // OneFromRulesRule：过 1/denom 后等权取一子树
286	      for (const r of rule.rules ?? []) walkDrop(r, out, gate / (rule.denom ?? 1), tag, cond);
287	      return;
288	    case 'noRepeat': // 不放回抽样池：恒掉 count 件（概率由池构成）
289	      for (const id of rule.ids ?? []) pushDrop(out, id, null, 1, 1, c, tag);
290	      break;
291	    case 'dropOneByOne': // 四塔碎片：12-20 块（参数未数值化，见 NpcDrops.dropOneByOne）
292	      pushDrop(out, rule.item, gate, 12, 20, c, tag);
293	      break;
294	    case 'mechSpawn': // 三王召唤物各 1/2500
295	      pushDrop(out, 556, gate / 2500, 1, 1, c, tag);
296	      pushDrop(out, 544, gate / 2500, 1, 1, c, tag);
297	      pushDrop(out, 557, gate / 2500, 1, 1, c, tag);
298	      break;
299	    case 'gate': { // LeadingConditionRule：条件沿 OnSuccess 链下传
300	      for (const ch of rule.chain ?? []) walkDrop(ch.rule, out, gate, tag, c);
301	      return;
302	    }
303	    case 'nothing': case 'listRef': case 'slimeBody': case 'statueMimic':
304	      return;
305	    default:
306	      return; // 未知 kind 静默跳过（掉落求值运行时另有 warnOnce 兜底）
307	  }
308	  for (const ch of rule.chain ?? []) walkDrop(ch.rule, out, gate, tag, cond);
309	}
310	
311	/** 展平某 NPC 的掉落规则树 → 展示行（去重 + 按掉率降序） */
312	export function flattenDropsForNpc(npcId: number): DropLine[] {
313	  const rules = dropRulesByNpc.get(npcId);
314	  if (!rules) return [];
315	  const out: DropLine[] = [];
316	  for (const r of rules) walkDrop(r, out, 1, null, null);
317	  const seen = new Set<string>();
318	  const deduped = out.filter((d) => {
319	    const k = `${d.item}|${d.chance}|${d.min}|${d.max}|${d.cond}|${d.tag}`;
320	    if (seen.has(k)) return false;
321	    seen.add(k);
322	    return true;
323	  });
324	  deduped.sort((a, b) => (b.chance ?? -1) - (a.chance ?? -1) || a.item - b.item);
325	  return deduped;
326	}
327	
328	export function dropRateText(chance: number): string {
329	  const pct = chance * 100;
330	  const s = pct >= 100 ? '100' : String(Math.round(pct * 100) / 100);
331	  return `${s}%`;
332	}
333	
334	function dropTagText(tag: string): string {
335	  const key = `Bestiary.ItemDropConditions.${tag}`;
336	  const t = Lang.has(key) ? Lang.text(key) : '';
337	  return t || tag;
338	}
339	function dropCondText(cond: string): string {
340	  const key = `Bestiary.ItemDropConditions.${cond}`;
341	  const t = Lang.has(key) ? Lang.text(key) : '';
342	  return t; // 原版空串条件（NotExpert 等）不显示
343	}

345	// ============ 出没环境 + 风味文本 + 头像背景（BestiaryDatabaseNPCsPopulator + BestiaryEntry） ============
346	
347	type SpawnJson = typeof spawnData;
348	/** SpawnCondition 静态声明（populator :14-302）。priority = filterIconFrame（标签图标帧，
349	 *  FilterProviderInfoElement :24-29）；text = DisplayTextPriority（风味回退权重）；
350	 *  bg/bgColor = 条目头像背景 MapBG 与着色（IBestiaryBackgroundImagePathAndColorProvider） */
351	interface SpawnConditionDef {
352	  key?: string;
353	  priority: number;
354	  text?: number;
355	  bg?: string;
356	  bgColor?: number[];
357	  overlay?: string;
358	  overlayColor?: number[];
359	  hideInPortrait?: boolean;
360	}
361	const SPAWN_CONDITIONS = (spawnData as unknown as SpawnJson).conditions as unknown as Record<string, SpawnConditionDef>;
362	/** FindEntryByNPCID(id).Info.AddRange 注册序（populator :802/:1661，×732） */
363	export const SPAWN_BY_NPC = (spawnData as unknown as SpawnJson).byNpc as Record<string, string[]>;
364	/** AddTags 头像背景偏好（populator :515-548）：单条件路径或腐化/猩红双路 */
365	const BG_PREFERENCE = (spawnData as unknown as SpawnJson).bgPreference as Record<
366	  string, Array<string | { corrupt: string; crimson: string }>
367	>;
368	/** MoonLordPortraitBackgroundProviderBestiaryInfoElement（populator :550-551） */
369	const MOONLORD_BG_NPC = new Set<number>(
370	  ((spawnData as unknown as SpawnJson).moonLordBg as number[] | undefined) ?? [],
371	);
372	
373	export interface SpawnConditionLine { key: string; label: string; priority: number }
374	
375	/** 出没环境条目（UIBestiaryEntryInfoPage 按注册序呈现；解锁门 NotKnown 之外恒显，
376	 *  FilterProviderInfoElement.cs:47-50）。netId 变种（-1..-13）按负 id 直查原表 */
377	export function bestiarySpawnConditions(npcId: number): SpawnConditionLine[] {
378	  const paths = SPAWN_BY_NPC[String(npcId)];
379	  if (!paths) return [];
380	  return paths.map((p) => {
381	    const c = SPAWN_CONDITIONS[p];
382	    const key = c.key ?? '';
383	    return { key, label: key && Lang.has(key) ? Lang.text(key) : key, priority: c.priority };
384	  });
385	}
386	
387	/** 条目背景偏好路径（BestiaryPortraitBackgroundBasedOnWorldEvil 按 WorldGen.crimson 二选一） */
388	export function bestiaryBgPreferencePaths(npcId: number, crimson = false): string[] {
389	  const prefs = BG_PREFERENCE[String(npcId)];
390	  if (!prefs) return [];
391	  return prefs.map((p) => (typeof p === 'string' ? p : (crimson ? p.crimson : p.corrupt)));
392	}
393	
394	/** 风味文本（BestiaryEntry.cs:42-47 Enemy/TownNPC/Critter 三工厂同构）：
395	 *  `Bestiary_FlavorText.npc_<NPCName 内部名>`（Language.Exists 门，564 键）；
396	 *  缺失回退 TryGivingEntryFlavorTextIfItIsMissing（populator:685-714）——头像背景
397	 *  偏好条件绝对优先（Info 序首个），其余取 DisplayTextPriority 最高者（并列取注册序
398	 *  后者），映射 `Bestiary_BiomeText.biome_<键名去类别>`。两路都无 → null */
399	export function bestiaryFlavorText(npcId: number, crimson = false): string | null {
400	  const internal = NPC_NAME_BY_ID[npcId > 0 ? npcId : -npcId];
401	  const key = internal ? `Bestiary_FlavorText.npc_${internal}` : '';
402	  if (key && Lang.has(key)) return Lang.text(key);
403	  const paths = SPAWN_BY_NPC[String(npcId)] ?? [];
404	  if (paths.length === 0) return null;
405	  const pref = bestiaryBgPreferencePaths(npcId, crimson);
406	  let bestPath: string | null = null;
407	  if (pref.length) {
408	    bestPath = paths.find((p) => pref.includes(p)) ?? null;   // Info 序首个命中即 break
409	  }
410	  if (!bestPath) {
411	    let bestPri = -Infinity;
412	    for (const p of paths) {
413	      const pri = SPAWN_CONDITIONS[p]?.text ?? 0;   // DisplayTextPriority 默认 0
414	      if (pri >= bestPri) { bestPri = pri; bestPath = p; }
415	    }
416	  }
417	  const cond = bestPath ? SPAWN_CONDITIONS[bestPath] : null;
418	  if (!cond?.key) return null;
419	  const fb = `Bestiary_BiomeText.biome_${cond.key.slice(cond.key.indexOf('.') + 1)}`;
420	  return Lang.has(fb) ? Lang.text(fb) : null;
421	}
422	
423	// ============ 头像背景（MapBG 按出没环境推导） ============
424	
425	export interface BestiaryPortraitBg {
426	  /** MapBG 贴图名（public/sprites/vanilla/<name>.png；无出没环境时 null） */
427	  image: string | null;
428	  /** 着色 rgba（NPCPortraitInfoElement：有图条件中最后一个带色者胜出） */
429	  color: [number, number, number, number];
430	}
431	
432	/** 条目 Info 中带背景图的提供者（注册序；月总元素由 populator :551 追加在末位） */
433	interface BgProvider { path: string | null; bg: string; bgColor?: number[] }
434	function bgProviders(npcId: number): BgProvider[] {
435	  const out: BgProvider[] = [];
436	  for (const p of SPAWN_BY_NPC[String(npcId)] ?? []) {
437	    const c = SPAWN_CONDITIONS[p];
438	    if (c?.bg) out.push({ path: p, bg: c.bg, bgColor: c.bgColor });
439	  }
440	  if (MOONLORD_BG_NPC.has(npcId)) out.push({ path: null, bg: 'MapBG1', bgColor: [0, 0, 0, 255] });
441	  return out;
442	}
443	
444	/** 详情页大头像背景（NPCPortraitInfoElement.cs:38-81）：偏好条件（AddTags，
445	 *  世界恶双路按 crimson 取一）命中则锁定其贴图；否则每轮覆写 → 注册序**最后一个**
446	 *  带图条件。着色 = 有图提供者中最后一个声明 bgColor 者（偏好命中后仍生效） */
447	export function bestiaryPortraitBackground(npcId: number, crimson = false): BestiaryPortraitBg {
448	  const providers = bgProviders(npcId);
449	  let image: string | null = null;
450	  let color: [number, number, number, number] = [255, 255, 255, 255];
451	  const pref = bestiaryBgPreferencePaths(npcId, crimson);
452	  const prefHit = pref.length ? providers.find((p) => p.path !== null && pref.includes(p.path)) : undefined;
453	  if (prefHit) image = prefHit.bg;                       // flag2：偏好锁定
454	  for (const p of providers) {
455	    if (!prefHit) image = p.bg;                          // 无偏好：末个胜出
456	    if (p.bgColor) color = [p.bgColor[0], p.bgColor[1], p.bgColor[2], p.bgColor[3] ?? 255];
457	  }
458	  return { image, color };
459	}
460	
461	/** 网格条目底图（UIBestiaryEntryButton.TryGettingBackgroundImageProvider :105-127）：
462	 *  偏好条件优先，否则注册序**首个**带图条件（与大头像的"最后一个"不同）；无着色 */
463	export function bestiaryGridBackground(npcId: number, crimson = false): string | null {
464	  const providers = bgProviders(npcId);
465	  if (!providers.length) return null;
466	  const pref = bestiaryBgPreferencePaths(npcId, crimson);
467	  const hit = pref.length ? providers.find((p) => p.path !== null && pref.includes(p.path)) : undefined;
468	  return (hit ?? providers[0]).bg;
469	}
470	
471	// ============ DOM 面板 ============
472	
473	const CSS = `

475	  width:min(1040px,96vw); height:min(740px,92vh); z-index:30; cursor:auto;

487	  border-radius:4px; padding:4px 6px; font-family:inherit; font-size:12px; }

489	.sw-bst-chips { display:flex; gap:4px; flex:1; flex-wrap:wrap; }
490	.sw-bst-chip { padding:4px 8px; font-size:11px; background:#232c52; border:1px solid #3a4680;
491	  border-radius:4px; cursor:pointer; color:#cfd6f2; font-family:inherit; }
492	.sw-bst-chip.active { background:#5a6ac0; color:#fff; outline:1px solid #ffd76e; }
493	.sw-bst-back { flex:none; padding:5px 14px; font-size:12px; }
494	.sw-bst-main { flex:1; display:flex; gap:8px; padding:0 10px; min-height:0; }
495	.sw-bst-gridwrap { flex:1; min-width:0; position:relative; overflow:hidden; }
496	.sw-bst-grid { display:grid; }
497	.sw-bst-cell { width:72px; height:72px; position:relative; flex:none;
498	  display:flex; flex-direction:column; align-items:center; justify-content:center; gap:1px;
499	  cursor:pointer; background:rgba(20,25,50,0.85); border-radius:3px; }
500	.sw-bst-cell:hover { background:rgba(42,52,100,1); outline:1px solid #8fa0e0; }
501	.sw-bst-cell.sel { outline:2px solid #ffd76e; }

503	.sw-bst-cell.sw-bst-lock canvas { filter:brightness(0) opacity(0.55); }
504	.sw-bst-name { font-size:10px; line-height:1.1; max-width:68px; white-space:nowrap;
505	  overflow:hidden; text-overflow:ellipsis; color:#cfd6f2; }
506	.sw-bst-cell.sw-bst-lock .sw-bst-name { color:#586088; }
507	.sw-bst-kills { position:absolute; right:3px; top:2px; font-size:9px; color:#ffe8a0; }
508	.sw-bst-empty { position:absolute; inset:0; display:flex; align-items:center;
509	  justify-content:center; color:#8a90b8; font-size:13px; }
510	.sw-bst-info { width:296px; flex:none; overflow-y:auto; background:rgba(20,25,50,0.8);
511	  border:1px solid #3a4680; border-radius:4px; padding:10px; font-size:12px; }
512	.sw-bst-info h3 { margin:0 0 4px; font-size:15px; color:#ffe8a0; font-weight:normal;
513	  text-shadow:1px 1px 0 #000; word-break:break-all; }
514	.sw-bst-tags { display:flex; gap:4px; flex-wrap:wrap; margin-bottom:6px; }
515	.sw-bst-tag { font-size:10px; padding:1px 6px; border-radius:3px; background:#3a4680; color:#cfd6f2; }
516	.sw-bst-tag.boss { background:#7a3a3a; }

518	  padding:2px 0; border-bottom:1px solid rgba(90,106,160,0.25); }
519	.sw-bst-info .row span:first-child { color:#8a90b8; }
520	.sw-bst-info .row span:last-child { color:#e8ecf8; }

523	.sw-bst-drop canvas { width:22px; height:22px; flex:none; image-rendering:pixelated; }
524	.sw-bst-drop .nm { flex:1; min-width:0; overflow:hidden; text-overflow:ellipsis; white-space:nowrap; }
525	.sw-bst-drop .rt { color:#9adf9a; flex:none; }
526	.sw-bst-drop .cd { display:block; font-size:10px; color:#8a90b8; }
527	.sw-bst-bottom { display:flex; align-items:center; gap:10px; padding:6px 10px 10px; flex:none; }
528	.sw-bst-pct { flex:none; font-size:12px; color:#cfd6f2; }
529	.sw-bst-bar { flex:1; height:15px; background:rgb(35,43,81); border-radius:3px; overflow:hidden; }

532	
533	export interface BestiaryPanelFlow {
534	  getTracker(): BestiaryTracker;
535	  atlas?: SpriteAtlas | null;
536	  /** 世界恶取向（BestiaryPortraitBackgroundBasedOnWorldEvil：crimson=true 取猩红路） */
537	  isCrimson?(): boolean;
538	  onBack(): void;
539	}
540	
541	export class BestiaryPanel {
542	  private panel: HTMLElement;
543	  private gridWrap!: HTMLElement;
544	  private gridEl!: HTMLElement;
545	  private rangeEl!: HTMLElement;
546	  private infoEl!: HTMLElement;
547	  private pctEl!: HTMLElement;
548	  private barEl!: HTMLElement;
549	  private searchEl!: HTMLInputElement;
550	  private chipsEl!: HTMLElement;
551	  private ro: ResizeObserver | null = null;
552	  private escHandler = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
553	    if (e.code === 'Escape' || e.code === 'KeyE') {
554	      e.stopImmediatePropagation();
555	      e.preventDefault();
556	      this.close();
557	    }
558	  };
559	  private filter: BestiaryFilter = { ...DEFAULT_BESTIARY_FILTER };
560	  private sortKey: BestiarySortKey = 'unlocks';
561	  private offset = 0;
562	  private perPage = 40;
563	  private working: BestiaryRow[] = [];
564	  private selected: BestiaryRow | null = null;
565	  private sortSel: HTMLSelectElement | null = null;
566	
567	  constructor(private parent: HTMLElement, private flow: BestiaryPanelFlow) {
568	    if (!document.getElementById('sw-bst-style')) {
569	      const style = document.createElement('style');
570	      style.id = 'sw-bst-style';
571	      style.textContent = CSS;
572	      document.head.appendChild(style);
573	    }
574	    this.panel = document.createElement('div');
575	    this.panel.className = 'sw-bst';
576	    this.panel.innerHTML = `
577	      <div class="sw-bst-top">
578	        <button class="sw-bst-nav" data-nav="-1">◀</button>
579	        <button class="sw-bst-nav" data-nav="1">▶</button>
580	        <div class="sw-bst-range">0-0 (0)</div>
581	        <select class="sw-bst-sel" data-sort></select>
582	        <div class="sw-bst-chips"></div>
583	        <input class="sw-bst-search" type="text" placeholder="搜索" data-search />
584	        <button class="sw-bst-nav sw-bst-back" data-close></button>
585	      </div>
586	      <div class="sw-bst-main">
587	        <div class="sw-bst-gridwrap"><div class="sw-bst-grid"></div><div class="sw-bst-empty" hidden></div></div>
588	        <div class="sw-bst-info"></div>
589	      </div>
590	      <div class="sw-bst-bottom">
591	        <div class="sw-bst-pct"></div>
592	        <div class="sw-bst-bar"><i style="width:0%"></i></div>
593	      </div>
594	    `;
595	    this.gridWrap = this.panel.querySelector('.sw-bst-gridwrap')!;
596	    this.gridEl = this.panel.querySelector('.sw-bst-grid')!;
597	    this.rangeEl = this.panel.querySelector('.sw-bst-range')!;
598	    this.infoEl = this.panel.querySelector('.sw-bst-info')!;
599	    this.pctEl = this.panel.querySelector('.sw-bst-pct')!;
600	    this.barEl = this.panel.querySelector('.sw-bst-bar i') as HTMLElement;
601	    this.searchEl = this.panel.querySelector('[data-search]') as HTMLInputElement;
602	    this.chipsEl = this.panel.querySelector('.sw-bst-chips')!;
603	    this.sortSel = this.panel.querySelector('[data-sort]') as HTMLSelectElement;
604	    this.buildSortSel();
605	    this.buildChips();
606	    this.panel.querySelector('[data-close]')!.textContent = Lang.text('UI.Back');
607	    this.panel.querySelector('[data-close]')!.addEventListener('click', () => this.close());
608	    this.panel.querySelectorAll('[data-nav]').forEach((b) => {
609	      (b as HTMLElement).addEventListener('click', () => this.page(Number((b as HTMLElement).dataset.nav)));
610	    });
611	    this.searchEl.addEventListener('input', () => { this.filter.search = this.searchEl.value; this.offset = 0; this.refresh(); });
612	    this.sortSel.addEventListener('change', () => {
613	      this.sortKey = (this.sortSel!.value || 'unlocks') as BestiarySortKey;
614	      this.offset = 0;
615	      this.refresh();
616	    });
617	    // capture 阶段拦截：防 main.ts 全局 Esc/E 处理器同时关暂停菜单/背包
618	    window.addEventListener('keydown', this.escHandler, true);
619	    if (typeof ResizeObserver !== 'undefined') {
620	      this.ro = new ResizeObserver(() => this.refresh());
621	      this.ro.observe(this.gridWrap);
622	    }
623	    parent.appendChild(this.panel);
624	    this.refresh();
625	    UISfx.click();
626	  }
627	
628	  private buildSortSel() {
629	    if (!this.sortSel) return;
630	    for (const k of BESTIARY_SORT_KEYS) {
631	      const o = document.createElement('option');
632	      o.value = k;
633	      o.textContent = sortLabel(k);
634	      this.sortSel.appendChild(o);
635	    }
636	    this.sortSel.value = this.sortKey;
637	  }
638	
639	  private chip(label: string, active: boolean, fn: () => void): HTMLElement {
640	    const b = document.createElement('button');
641	    b.className = 'sw-bst-chip' + (active ? ' active' : '');
642	    b.textContent = label;
643	    b.addEventListener('click', () => { fn(); this.offset = 0; this.refresh(); });
644	    return b;
645	  }
646	
647	  private buildChips() {
648	    const f = this.filter;
649	    const kinds: Array<BestiaryFilter['kind']> = ['all', 'enemy', 'town', 'critter'];
650	    const kindLabel: Record<string, string> = { all: '全部', enemy: '敌怪', town: '城镇', critter: '小动物' };
651	    this.chipsEl.replaceChildren();
652	    for (const k of kinds) {
653	      this.chipsEl.appendChild(this.chip(kindLabel[k], f.kind === k, () => { f.kind = k; this.buildChips(); }));
654	    }
655	    this.chipsEl.appendChild(this.chip(
656	      Lang.text('BestiaryInfo.IsBoss'), f.boss, () => { f.boss = !f.boss; this.buildChips(); }));
657	    const ul = f.unlocked;
658	    this.chipsEl.appendChild(this.chip(
659	      Lang.text('BestiaryInfo.IfUnlocked'), ul === 'yes', () => { f.unlocked = ul === 'yes' ? 'all' : 'yes'; this.buildChips(); }));
660	    this.chipsEl.appendChild(this.chip(
661	      '未解锁', ul === 'no', () => { f.unlocked = ul === 'no' ? 'all' : 'no'; this.buildChips(); }));
662	  }
663	
664	  /** OnOpenPage → UpdateBestiaryContents（FilterEntries → SortEntries → Fill + 进度） */
665	  refresh() {
666	    const rows = bestiaryRows(this.flow.getTracker());
667	    sortBestiaryRows(rows, this.sortKey);
668	    this.working = filterBestiaryRows(rows, this.filter);
669	    const { cols, rows: gridRows, perPage } = bestiaryGridSize(this.gridWrap.clientWidth, this.gridWrap.clientHeight);
670	    this.perPage = perPage;
671	    this.gridEl.style.gridTemplateColumns = `repeat(${cols}, 72px)`;
672	    this.offset = clampBestiaryOffset(this.offset, 0, this.working.length, perPage);
673	    this.rangeEl.textContent = bestiaryRangeText(this.offset, this.working.length, perPage);
674	    void gridRows;
675	    this.fillGrid();
676	    this.updateProgress();
677	    this.renderInfo();
678	  }
679	
680	  private page(dir: number) {
681	    this.offset = clampBestiaryOffset(this.offset, dir * this.perPage, this.working.length, this.perPage);
682	    this.refresh();
683	  }
684	
685	  private fillGrid() {
686	    this.gridEl.replaceChildren();
687	    const empty = this.gridWrap.querySelector('.sw-bst-empty') as HTMLElement;
688	    if (this.working.length === 0) {
689	      empty.hidden = false;
690	      return;
691	    }
692	    empty.hidden = true;
693	    const end = Math.min(this.working.length, this.offset + this.perPage);
694	    for (let i = this.offset; i < end; i++) {
695	      const r = this.working[i];
696	      const cell = document.createElement('div');
697	      cell.className = 'sw-bst-cell' + (r.state === UnlockState.NotKnown ? ' sw-bst-lock' : '');
698	      if (this.selected && this.selected.entry.creditId === r.entry.creditId) cell.classList.add('sel');
699	      cell.dataset.credit = r.entry.creditId;
700	      const canvas = document.createElement('canvas');
701	      canvas.width = 64; canvas.height = 64;
702	      cell.appendChild(canvas);
703	      const name = document.createElement('div');
704	      name.className = 'sw-bst-name';
705	      name.textContent = r.name;
706	      cell.appendChild(name);
707	      if (r.kills > 0 && r.state >= UnlockState.PortraitOnly) {
708	        const kills = document.createElement('div');
709	        kills.className = 'sw-bst-kills';
710	        kills.textContent = String(r.kills);
711	        cell.appendChild(kills);
712	      }
713	      cell.addEventListener('click', () => { this.selected = r; this.refresh(); });
714	      this.drawPortrait(canvas, r.npcId, 'grid', r.state > UnlockState.NotKnown);
715	      this.gridEl.appendChild(cell);
716	    }
717	  }
718	
719	  /** 头像 = NPC 表首帧（UnlockableNPCEntryIcon 用整表绘制；DOM 取 frame0 缩放）。
720	   *  负 netID 变体（史莱姆配色 -1..-10 / 世吞段 -11..-13）无独立贴图文件，
721	   *  原版共用母体表 → 分别回落 NPC_1 / NPC_14。
722	   *  已解锁条目先铺 MapBG 出没环境底图（UIBestiaryEntryButton 网格 / NPCPortraitInfoElement
723	   *  详情各取一套推导），着色按原版 bgColor 乘算（multiply）。 */
724	  private drawPortrait(canvas: HTMLCanvasElement, npcId: number, bgMode: 'grid' | 'portrait', unlocked: boolean) {
725	    const sheetId = npcId > 0 ? npcId : (npcId >= -10 ? 1 : 14);
726	    const crimson = this.flow.isCrimson?.() ?? false;
727	    const bg = unlocked
728	      ? (bgMode === 'grid'
729	        ? { image: bestiaryGridBackground(npcId, crimson), color: [255, 255, 255, 255] as number[] }
730	        : bestiaryPortraitBackground(npcId, crimson))
731	      : { image: null as string | null, color: [255, 255, 255, 255] };
732	    const size = canvas.width;
733	    const paint = (img: HTMLImageElement) => {
734	      const frames = VANILLA_NPCS[String(npcId)]?.frames ?? 1;
735	      const fh = Math.max(1, Math.floor(img.height / frames));
736	      const cx = canvas.getContext('2d');
737	      if (!cx) return;
738	      cx.imageSmoothingEnabled = false;
739	      cx.clearRect(0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);
740	      const s = Math.min(size / img.width, size / fh);
741	      const w = img.width * s, h = fh * s;
742	      cx.drawImage(img, 0, 0, img.width, fh, (size - w) / 2, (size - h) / 2, w, h);
743	    };
744	    const atlas = this.flow.atlas ?? null;
745	    const loadBg = (name: string, cb: (bgi: HTMLImageElement) => void) => {
746	      const cached = atlas ? atlas.vimages.get(`vanilla/${name}.png`) ?? null : null;
747	      if (cached) { cb(cached); return; }
748	      const bgi = new Image();
749	      bgi.onload = () => cb(bgi);
750	      bgi.src = `sprites/vanilla/${name}.png`;
751	    };
752	    const paintWithBg = (bgImg: HTMLImageElement | null) => {
753	      const cx = canvas.getContext('2d');
754	      if (!cx) return;
755	      cx.imageSmoothingEnabled = false;
756	      cx.clearRect(0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);
757	      if (bgImg) {
758	        const bs = Math.max(size / bgImg.width, size / bgImg.height);
759	        const bw = bgImg.width * bs, bh = bgImg.height * bs;
760	        cx.drawImage(bgImg, (size - bw) / 2, (size - bh) / 2, bw, bh);
761	        const [r, g, b, a] = bg.color;
762	        if (r !== 255 || g !== 255 || b !== 255) {
763	          cx.globalCompositeOperation = 'multiply';
764	          cx.fillStyle = `rgb(${r},${g},${b})`;
765	          cx.fillRect(0, 0, size, size);
766	          cx.globalCompositeOperation = 'source-over';
767	          void a;
768	        }
769	      }
770	    };
771	    if (bg.image) {
772	      loadBg(bg.image, (bgImg) => {
773	        paintWithBg(bgImg);
774	        // NPC 表到位后叠画（贴图晚到时重绘底图再叠）
775	        const cached = atlas ? atlas.vimages.get(`vanilla/NPC_${sheetId}.png`) ?? null : null;
776	        if (cached) { paint(cached); return; }
777	        const img = new Image();
778	        img.onload = () => { paintWithBg(bgImg); paint(img); };
779	        img.src = `sprites/vanilla/NPC_${sheetId}.png`;
780	      });
781	      return;
782	    }
783	    const cached = atlas ? atlas.vimages.get(`vanilla/NPC_${sheetId}.png`) ?? null : null;
784	    if (cached) { paint(cached); return; }
785	    const img = new Image();
786	    img.onload = () => paint(img);
787	    img.src = `sprites/vanilla/NPC_${sheetId}.png`;
788	  }
789	
790	  /** 掉落行物品小图标（atlas.vicon 懒载未就绪时仅文字） */
791	  private itemIcon(itemId: number): HTMLCanvasElement | null {
792	    const atlas = this.flow.atlas ?? null;
793	    if (!atlas) return null;
794	    const ar = atlas.vicon(itemId);
795	    if (!ar) return null;
796	    const c = document.createElement('canvas');
797	    c.width = 22; c.height = 22;
798	    const cx = c.getContext('2d')!;
799	    cx.imageSmoothingEnabled = false;
800	    const s = Math.min(22 / ar.sw, 22 / ar.sh);
801	    const w = ar.sw * s, h = ar.sh * s;
802	    cx.drawImage(ar.img, ar.sx, ar.sy, ar.sw, ar.sh, (22 - w) / 2, (22 - h) / 2, w, h);
803	    return c;
804	  }
805	
806	  private updateProgress() {
807	    const c = this.flow.getTracker().completion();
808	    const pct = Math.round(c.percent * 10000) / 100;
809	    const txt = Lang.text('BestiaryInfo.PercentCollected').replace('{Percent}', `${pct}%`);
810	    this.pctEl.textContent = txt;
811	    this.pctEl.title = `${c.unlocked}/${c.total}`;
812	    this.barEl.style.width = `${c.percent * 100}%`;
813	  }
814	
815	  /** 右侧详情栏（UIBestiaryEntryInfoPage.FillInfoForEntry；未选时空白态） */
816	  private renderInfo() {
817	    const r = this.selected;
818	    if (!r) {
819	      this.infoEl.replaceChildren();
820	      const hint = document.createElement('div');
821	      hint.style.cssText = 'color:#8a90b8;text-align:center;margin-top:24px;';
822	      hint.textContent = '点击条目查看详情';
823	      this.infoEl.appendChild(hint);
824	      return;
825	    }
826	    const st = r.state;
827	    this.infoEl.replaceChildren();
828	    const h3 = document.createElement('h3');
829	    h3.textContent = st > UnlockState.NotKnown ? r.name : '???';
830	    this.infoEl.appendChild(h3);
831	    const tags = document.createElement('div');
832	    tags.className = 'sw-bst-tags';
833	    const mkTag = (label: string, boss = false) => {
834	      const t = document.createElement('span');
835	      t.className = 'sw-bst-tag' + (boss ? ' boss' : '');
836	      t.textContent = label;
837	      tags.appendChild(t);
838	    };
839	    mkTag(st > UnlockState.NotKnown ? Lang.text('BestiaryInfo.IfUnlocked') : '未解锁');
840	    if (isBossCredit(r.entry.creditId)) mkTag(Lang.text('BestiaryInfo.IsBoss'), true);
841	    if (r.entry.kind === 'critter') mkTag('小动物');
842	    if (r.entry.goldGate) mkTag('金小动物');
843	    this.infoEl.appendChild(tags);
844	    // 出没环境（SpawnConditionBestiaryInfoElement 族，populator :802/:1661 注册序；
845	    // 解锁门 = 非 NotKnown，FilterProviderInfoElement.cs:47-50——比属性更早可见）
846	    if (st > UnlockState.NotKnown) {
847	      const conds = bestiarySpawnConditions(r.npcId);
848	      if (conds.length) {
849	        const sec = document.createElement('div');
850	        sec.className = 'sw-bst-sec';
851	        sec.textContent = '出没环境'; // 原版信息页分区无独立 lang 键（与"掉落"同处理）
852	        this.infoEl.appendChild(sec);
853	        const wrap = document.createElement('div');
854	        wrap.className = 'sw-bst-tags';
855	        for (const c of conds) {
856	          const t = document.createElement('span');
857	          t.className = 'sw-bst-tag';
858	          t.textContent = c.label;
859	          wrap.appendChild(t);
860	        }
861	        this.infoEl.appendChild(wrap);
862	      }
863	    }
864	    // 大头像
865	    const canvas = document.createElement('canvas');
866	    canvas.width = 96; canvas.height = 96;
867	    canvas.style.cssText = 'width:96px;height:96px;image-rendering:pixelated;display:block;margin:0 auto 6px;';
868	    if (st === UnlockState.NotKnown) canvas.style.filter = 'brightness(0) opacity(0.55)';
869	    this.infoEl.appendChild(canvas);
870	    this.drawPortrait(canvas, r.npcId, 'portrait', st > UnlockState.NotKnown);
871	    // 击杀数（NPCKillCounterInfoElement）
872	    if (r.kills > 0 && st >= UnlockState.PortraitOnly) {
873	      const row = this.infoRow(Lang.text('BestiaryInfo.Slain'), String(r.kills));
874	      this.infoEl.appendChild(row);
875	    }
876	    // 风味文本（FlavorTextBestiaryInfoElement：解锁 ≥ Stats 才呈现，:21-24）：
877	    // `Bestiary_FlavorText.npc_<名>` 直取，缺失回退最高优先出没环境的 biome 文本
878	    if (st >= UnlockState.Stats) {
879	      const flavor = bestiaryFlavorText(r.npcId, this.flow.isCrimson?.() ?? false);
880	      if (flavor) {
881	        const p = document.createElement('p');
882	        p.style.cssText = 'margin:6px 0 2px;font-size:11px;line-height:1.5;color:#cfd6f2;'
883	          + 'background:rgba(43,56,101,0.55);border-radius:4px;padding:6px;';
884	        p.textContent = flavor;
885	        this.infoEl.appendChild(p);
886	      }
887	    }
888	    // 属性（NPCStatsReportInfoElement：UnlockState ≥ Stats 才可见）
889	    if (st >= UnlockState.Stats) {
890	      // 稀有度星（ContentSamples.NpcBestiaryRarityStars：0-5；原版以星形图标列呈现，
891	      // 此处以文本星近似并按星数着色 1-5 灰→金）
892	      const stars = bestiaryRarityStars(r.npcId);
893	      if (stars > 0) {
894	        const starRow = this.infoRow('稀有度', '★'.repeat(stars));
895	        starRow.style.color = stars >= 4 ? '#e8c05a' : stars >= 2 ? '#b8c0d8' : '#8a90b8';
896	        this.infoEl.appendChild(starRow);
897	      }
898	      const v = VANILLA_NPCS[String(r.npcId)];
899	      if (v) {
900	        this.infoEl.appendChild(this.infoRow(Lang.text('BestiaryInfo.Life'), String(v.lifeMax)));
901	        this.infoEl.appendChild(this.infoRow(Lang.text('BestiaryInfo.Attack'), String(v.damage)));
902	        this.infoEl.appendChild(this.infoRow(Lang.text('BestiaryInfo.Defense'), String(v.defense)));
903	        const kb = Math.round(v.knockBackResist * 100);
904	        this.infoEl.appendChild(this.infoRow(Lang.text('BestiaryInfo.Knockback'), `${kb}%`));
905	      }
906	    }
907	    // 掉落表（ItemDropBestiaryInfoElement：≥ DropsNoRates 列表，≥ DropsWithRates 带掉率）
908	    if (st >= UnlockState.DropsNoRates) {
909	      const sec = document.createElement('div');
910	      sec.className = 'sw-bst-sec';
911	      sec.textContent = '掉落';
912	      this.infoEl.appendChild(sec);
913	      const drops = flattenDropsForNpc(r.npcId);
914	      if (drops.length === 0) {
915	        const none = document.createElement('div');
916	        none.style.cssText = 'color:#8a90b8;';
917	        none.textContent = '（无掉落记录）';
918	        this.infoEl.appendChild(none);
919	      }
920	      const shown = drops.slice(0, 30);
921	      for (const d of shown) {
922	        const line = document.createElement('div');
923	        line.className = 'sw-bst-drop';
924	        const icon = this.itemIcon(d.item);
925	        if (icon) line.appendChild(icon);
926	        const nm = document.createElement('span');
927	        nm.className = 'nm';
928	        const iname = Lang.itemName(d.item) ?? String(d.item);
929	        nm.textContent = iname + (d.min !== d.max ? ` ×${d.min}-${d.max}` : d.min > 1 ? ` ×${d.min}` : '');
930	        const notes = [d.cond ? dropCondText(d.cond) : '', d.tag ? dropTagText(d.tag) : ''].filter(Boolean);
931	        if (notes.length) {
932	          const cd = document.createElement('span');
933	          cd.className = 'cd';
934	          cd.textContent = notes.join(' · ');
935	          nm.appendChild(cd);
936	        }
937	        line.appendChild(nm);
938	        if (st >= UnlockState.DropsWithRates && d.chance !== null) {
939	          const rt = document.createElement('span');
940	          rt.className = 'rt';
941	          rt.textContent = dropRateText(d.chance);
942	          line.appendChild(rt);
943	        }
944	        this.infoEl.appendChild(line);
945	      }
946	      if (drops.length > shown.length) {
947	        const more = document.createElement('div');
948	        more.style.cssText = 'color:#8a90b8;';
949	        more.textContent = `…另有 ${drops.length - shown.length} 条`;
950	        this.infoEl.appendChild(more);
951	      }
952	    } else if (st === UnlockState.PortraitOnly) {
953	      const hint = document.createElement('div');
954	      hint.style.cssText = 'color:#8a90b8;margin-top:8px;';
955	      hint.textContent = r.entry.kind === 'town'
956	        ? '与该 NPC 对话可记录更多信息'
957	        : '继续击杀可解锁属性与掉落信息';
958	      this.infoEl.appendChild(hint);
959	    } else {
960	      const hint = document.createElement('div');
961	      hint.style.cssText = 'color:#8a90b8;margin-top:8px;';
962	      hint.textContent = r.entry.kind === 'town'
963	        ? '与该 NPC 对话以记录'
964	        : r.entry.kind === 'critter' ? '发现该生物以记录' : '击败该怪物以记录';
965	      this.infoEl.appendChild(hint);
966	    }
967	  }
968	
969	  private infoRow(label: string, value: string): HTMLElement {
970	    const row = document.createElement('div');
971	    row.className = 'row';
972	    const a = document.createElement('span');
973	    a.textContent = label;
974	    const b = document.createElement('span');
975	    b.textContent = value;
976	    row.append(a, b);
977	    return row;
978	  }
979	
980	  close() {
981	    window.removeEventListener('keydown', this.escHandler, true);
982	    this.ro?.disconnect();
983	    this.ro = null;
984	    this.selected = null;
985	    this.panel.remove();
986	    this.flow.onBack();
987	  }
988	}
```

```
35	  valueToCoins(value: number): string {

45	    if (silver > 0) text += `${silver} ${Lang.text('Currency.Silver').toLowerCase()} `;
46	    if (copper > 0) text += `${copper} ${Lang.text('Currency.Copper').toLowerCase()} `;
47	    if (text.length > 0) text = text.slice(0, -1);

145	  /** 投射物名(Lang.cs:444 GetProjectileName;ProjectileName 在 Projectiles 分片) */
146	  projectileName(id: number): string | null {
147	    const internal = PROJECTILE_NAME_BY_ID[id];
148	    return internal && Lang.has(`ProjectileName.${internal}`) ? Lang.text(`ProjectileName.${internal}`) : null;
149	  },
150	
151	  /**
152	   * 图块名。解析链(1.4.4+ 原版语义:方块无独立显示名——Tiles 分节为空)：
153	   * ① 放置它的物品名(TILE_NAME_ITEM_BY_SHEET:Item.createTile 反查,取基础款最小 id；
154	   *    如 tile14 表 → 木桌 WoodenTable)——12 语言官方译名权威来源
155	   * ② MapObject 族名(Lang.cs:77 GetMapObjectName 系,仅 79 键,族级泛称如"桌子")
156	   * ③ id-maps 方块名(TILE_NAME_ZH/EN_BY_ID：世界生成专属块——树/藤蔓/药草等
157	   *    无放置物品且 MapObject 缺失；原版对它们无 UI 显示名,此为唯一译名来源。
158	   *    zh 系语言取 zh,其余语言取 en)
159	   * ④ null(调用方回退 TILE_DEFS[].name)
160	   */
161	  tileName(id: number): string | null {
162	    const placer = TILE_NAME_ITEM_BY_SHEET[id];
163	    if (placer !== undefined) {
164	      const n = Lang.itemName(Number(placer));
165	      if (n) return n;
166	    }
167	    const internal = TILE_NAME_BY_ID[id];
168	    if (internal && Lang.has(`MapObject.${internal}`)) return Lang.text(`MapObject.${internal}`);
169	    const isZh = (languageManager.activeCulture?.name ?? '').startsWith('zh');
170	    const fallback = isZh ? TILE_NAME_ZH_BY_ID[id] : TILE_NAME_EN_BY_ID[id];
171	    return fallback ?? null;
172	  },
173	
174	  /**
175	   * 墙名。与 tileName 同构（原版墙无独立显示名）：
176	   * ① 放置它的物品名（WALL_NAME_ITEM_BY_WALL：Item.createWall 反查，取最小 id）
177	   * ② id-maps walls 的 zh/en（自然墙无放置物品——泥土墙/石墙等）
178	   * ③ null（调用方回退 WALL_DEFS[].name）
179	   */
180	  wallName(id: number): string | null {
181	    const placer = WALL_NAME_ITEM_BY_WALL[id];
182	    if (placer !== undefined) {
183	      const n = Lang.itemName(Number(placer));
184	      if (n) return n;
185	    }
186	    const isZh = (languageManager.activeCulture?.name ?? '').startsWith('zh');
187	    return (isZh ? WALL_NAME_ZH_BY_ID[id] : WALL_NAME_EN_BY_ID[id]) ?? null;
188	  },
189	
190	  /** 类别内取模循环取值(原版 IndexedFromCategory) */
191	  indexedFromCategory(category: string, index: number): string | null {
192	    return languageManager.indexedFromCategory(category, index);
193	  },
194	
195	  /** 类别内随机(原版 RandomFromCategory,如小贴士 LoadingTips_Default) */
196	  randomFromCategory(category: string, rng?: () => number): string | null {
197	    return languageManager.randomFromCategory(category, rng);
198	  },
199	};
200	

```

```
226	/** 按玩家环境取生成池 id 列表。biome：spawn 点地面 tile 判定的群系（Game 传入） */
227	export function poolFor(groundLevel: number, lavaLine: number, ty: number, isDay: boolean, biome?: string): number[] {
228	  if (debugPoolOverride) return debugPoolOverride;
229	  if (ty > lavaLine) return VANILLA_SPAWN_POOLS.hell;
230	  if (ty > groundLevel + 15) return VANILLA_SPAWN_POOLS.underground;
231	  if (biome && VANILLA_SPAWN_POOLS[biome as keyof typeof VANILLA_SPAWN_POOLS]) {
232	    return VANILLA_SPAWN_POOLS[biome as keyof typeof VANILLA_SPAWN_POOLS] as number[];
233	  }
234	  return isDay ? VANILLA_SPAWN_POOLS.daySurface : VANILLA_SPAWN_POOLS.nightSurface;
235	}

237	/** 按生成点地面 tile 判群系（原版 zone 判定的 tile 采样近似） */
238	export function biomeAt(st: { type: Uint16Array; idx(x: number, y: number): number; w: number; h: number }, tx: number, ty: number): string | null {
239	  // 从 ty 向下找第一个实心格
240	  for (let y = Math.max(2, ty); y < Math.min(st.h - 2, ty + 60); y++) {
241	    const t = st.type[st.idx(tx, y)];
242	    if (t === 0) continue;
243	    const key = TILE_KEY_NAME[t];
244	    if (!key) return null;
245	    if (key.includes('corrupt') || key === 'ebonstone_block' || key.includes('ebonsand')) return 'corruption';
246	    if (key.includes('crimson') || key.includes('crimsand') || key === 'crimstone') return 'crimson';
247	    if (key === 'mud') return 'jungle';
248	    if (key === 'ice' || key === 'snow') return 'snow';
249	    if (key === 'sand' || key === 'sandstone' || key === 'hardened_sand') return 'desert';
250	    return null;
251	  }
252	  return null;
253	}
```
