```
113	export function evalCond(name: string, args: unknown[], ctx: NpcDropCtx): boolean {
114	  const ex = ctx.extras;
115	  switch (name) {
116	    case 'NotExpert': return !ctx.isExpert;
117	    case 'IsExpert': return ctx.isExpert;
118	    // Conditions.cs:610-670（LegacyHack_IsBoss* = npc.boss && 档位）：
119	    // boss 位由 Enemy 死亡路径按 DropEoWLoot（NPC.cs:79212-79228）注入 ctx——
120	    // EoW 13/14/15 仅"场上再无同族"的最后一段为 true（宝袋/纪念章/宠物/魔矿
121	    // 20-60/面具/奖旗全挂此位），中间段恒 false 只出非门控小额
122	    case 'LegacyHack_IsBossAndExpert': return ctx.boss && ctx.isExpert;
123	    case 'LegacyHack_IsBossAndNotExpert': return ctx.boss && !ctx.isExpert;
124	    case 'LegacyHack_IsABoss': return ctx.boss;
125	    case 'Easymode': return !ctx.hardMode;
126	    case 'IsHardmode': return ctx.hardMode;
127	    case 'NotRemixSeedHardmode': return ctx.hardMode;
128	    case 'RemixSeedHardmode': return false;
129	    // Conditions.cs:1370-1394 / :1318-1342：!remixWorld && !hardMode / remixWorld && !hardMode。
130	    // remix 秘密种子未实装（remixWorld 恒 false）→ 前者退化为 !hardMode（同 NotRemixSeedHardmode
131	    // 的退化约定），后者恒 false。曾双双落 default → 冰模仿怪 629 肉前池链整支失效
132	    case 'NotRemixSeedEasymode': return !ctx.hardMode;
133	    case 'RemixSeedEasymode': return false;
134	    case 'RemixSeed': return false;
135	    case 'NotRemixSeed': return true;
136	    case 'NeverTrue': return false;
137	    case 'NotFromStatue': return !ctx.statueSpawned; // Conditions.NotFromStatue
138	    case 'IsBloodMoonAndNotFromStatue': return !!ex?.bloodMoon && !ctx.statueSpawned;
139	    case 'IsCrimsonAndNotExpert': return ctx.crimson && !ctx.isExpert;
140	    case 'IsCorruptionAndNotExpert': return !ctx.crimson && !ctx.isExpert;
141	    case 'IsChristmas': return !!ex?.xmas;
142	    case 'HalloweenWeapons': return !!ex?.halloween;
143	    case 'HalloweenGoodieBagDrop': case 'XmasPresentDrop': {
144	      // Conditions.cs:891-934：季节门之外还有 NPC 门——lifeMax>1 && damage>0 && !friendly
145	      // && type∉{121 墓穴爬虫,23 诅咒骷髅头} && value>0（曾只看季节，雕像怪 0 value 也会掉袋）
146	      const seasonal = name === 'HalloweenGoodieBagDrop' ? !!ex?.halloween : !!ex?.xmas;
147	      return seasonal && ctx.lifeMax > 1 && ctx.damage > 0 && !ctx.friendly
148	        && ctx.type !== 121 && ctx.type !== 23 && ctx.value > 0;
149	    }
150	    case 'DontStarveIsUp': return false;
151	    case 'TenthAnniversaryIsUp': return false;
152	    case 'DontStarveIsNotUp': case 'TenthAnniversaryIsNotUp': return true;
153	    case 'DownedPlantera': return downed(ctx, 262);
154	    case 'FirstTimeKillingPlantera': return !downed(ctx, 262); // 掉落先于 downed 写入 → 首杀语义
155	    case 'DownedAllMechBosses': return downed(ctx, 134, 125, 127);
156	    case 'BeatAnyMechBoss': return downed(ctx, 134) || downed(ctx, 125) || downed(ctx, 127);
157	    case 'MechdusaKill':
158	      // Conditions.cs:579：首需 SpecialSeedFeatures.Mechdusa（Mekdusa 秘密种子），
159	      // 再需三王一眼 AnyNPCs 扫描。秘密种子系统未实装 → 恒 false
160	      return false;
161	    case 'FromCertainWaveAndAbove': {
162	      // Conditions.cs:249-261：NPC.waveNumber >= neededWave（不查事件——无事件时
163	      // waveNumber=0 恒不满足；注册仅在冰女王专属掉落 1914 上，neededWave=15）
164	      return (ctx.moonEvent?.wave ?? 0) >= Number(args[0] ?? 0);
165	    }
166	    case 'PumpkinMoonDropGatingChance': case 'FrostMoonDropGatingChance': {
167	      // 稀有掉落总门（Conditions.cs:55-121）：霜月基数 28 / 南瓜月 24；
168	      // 专家 wave+5；denom = ⌊(base-wave)/2.5⌋，专家再 -2(霜)/-1(南瓜)；下限 1；
169	      // RollLuck(denom)==0 才放行（luck=0 ≡ rng.Next(denom)==0）
170	      const me = ctx.moonEvent;
171	      const frost = name === 'FrostMoonDropGatingChance';
172	      if (!me || me.kind !== (frost ? 1 : 2)) return false;
173	      let wave = me.wave;
174	      if (ctx.isExpert) wave += 5;
175	      let denom = Math.trunc(((frost ? 28 : 24) - wave) / 2.5);
176	      if (ctx.isExpert) denom -= frost ? 2 : 1;
177	      if (denom < 1) denom = 1;
178	      return rollLuck(ctx, denom, 1);
179	    }
180	    case 'PumpkinMoonDropGateForTrophies': case 'FrostMoonDropGateForTrophies': {
181	      // 纪念碑门（Conditions.cs:123-229）：wave≥15；denom = 4(15/16)/3(17/18)/2(19/20+)，
182	      // 专家 1/3 概率再 -1；info.rng.Next(denom)==0
183	      const me = ctx.moonEvent;
184	      const frost = name === 'FrostMoonDropGateForTrophies';
185	      if (!me || me.kind !== (frost ? 1 : 2) || me.wave < 15) return false;
186	      let denom = me.wave >= 19 ? 2 : me.wave >= 17 ? 3 : 4;
187	      if (ctx.isExpert && ctx.rng.int(0, 2) === 0) denom--;
188	      return ctx.rng.int(0, Math.max(1, denom) - 1) === 0;
189	    }
190	    case 'EmpressOfLightIsGenuinelyEnraged':
191	      // Conditions.cs:525 → NPC.cs AI_120_HallowBoss_IsGenuinelyEnraged(:46169)：
192	      // ai[3]∈{2,3}（白天暴怒/二阶段白天）。本作 ai3 与原版 ai[3] 同义
193	      // （bossAI_lategame.ts:370 IsInPhase2=ai3∈{1,3}），但白天暴怒分支未实现
194	      // （:554 仅置 1）→ 运行时恒 false，语义已 1:1 接通
195	      return ctx.type === 636 && (ctx.ai3 === 2 || ctx.ai3 === 3);
196	    case 'MissingTwin':
197	      // Conditions.cs:502：另一只眼（125↔126）不在场才放行——先死者不掉（NPC.AnyNPCs）
198	      return !!ctx.anyNpcOfType && !ctx.anyNpcOfType(ctx.type === 125 ? 126 : 125);
199	    case 'RedHatSkeletron':
200	      // Conditions.cs:543 → NPC.cs RedHatSkeletronAdjustmentsEnabled(:67282)：
201	      // 35/33 看 ai[3]==1（持有裁缝巫毒娃娃击杀裁缝召出的红帽骷髅王，NPC.cs:81203-81264），
202	      // 36/32 看 localAI[3]==1（引擎未承载 localAI[3] → 恒 false，均为不可达内容）
203	      return (ctx.type === 35 || ctx.type === 33) ? ctx.ai3 === 1
204	        : (ctx.type === 36 || ctx.type === 32) ? ctx.localAi3 === 1 : false;
205	    case 'SkyblockIsUp': case 'SkyblockIsUpNoSickle': return false;
206	    case 'WindyEnoughForKiteDrops': return !!ex?.windy;
207	    case 'EyeOfCthulhuDefeatedAndNoAltarsInWorld':
208	      // Conditions.cs:1422：NPC.downedBoss1 && WorldGen.Skyblock.noAltars。
209	      // flags.downedBoss1（downed_4? 否——Boss1=克眼）已有，但 noAltars 是天空盒世界
210	      // 专属字段、本作无天空盒世界 → 恒 false
211	      return false;
212	    case 'NamedNPC': return false; // 需 NPC.HasGivenName/GivenName 具名城镇 NPC
213	      // （Andrew/Whitney/Jim，ItemDropDatabase.cs:732-734）——具名系统未实装 → 恒 false
214	    case 'LivingFlames':
215	      // Conditions.cs:939 lifeMax>5 && value>0 && !friendly && hardMode && y>UnderworldLayer
216	      // && !IsInSimulation（后者仅图鉴模拟，运行时恒 false）——语义已 1:1
217	      return ctx.lifeMax > 5 && ctx.value > 0 && !ctx.friendly && ctx.hardMode

219	    case 'SoulOfLight': case 'SoulOfNight': {

287	// ============ 规则求值（ItemDropResolver.cs 语义） ============
288	/** Player.RollLuck(denom, luck=0) ≡ rng.Next(denom) */
289	function rollLuck(ctx: NpcDropCtx, denom: number, num: number): boolean {
290	  if (denom <= 1) return true;
291	  const luck = ctx.luck ?? 0;
292	  if (luck !== 0 && ctx.rng.next() < Math.abs(luck)) {
293	    // Luck.RollLuck（Luck.cs:6）：幸运 → 先掷 [denom/2, denom) 再掷（<num 更易命中）；
294	    // 负幸运反向 → [denom, denom*2) 更难命中
295	    const inner = luck > 0
296	      ? ctx.rng.int(Math.floor(denom / 2), denom - 1)
297	      : ctx.rng.int(denom, denom * 2 - 1);
298	    return ctx.rng.int(0, Math.max(1, inner) - 1) < num;
299	  }
300	  return ctx.rng.int(0, denom - 1) < num;
301	}
302	
303	/** 规则掷骰统一入口：JSON 的 noLuck 标记（NotScalingWithLuck 族）此前被忽略——
304	 *  CommonDropNotScalingWithLuck.cs:17 / OneFromOptionsNotScaledWithLuckDropRule.cs:115
305	 *  用 info.rng.Next(denom)<num（不进 Luck.RollLuck），必须走直掷而非 rollLuck，
306	 *  否则幸运玩家在这些规则上白吃加成（如 EoC 专家 Ore 池 NormalvsExpert…NotScalingWithLuck）。
307	 *  denom<=1 直接过与 rollLuck 同约定（结果恒真，仅差一次空抽） */
308	function tryRoll(ctx: NpcDropCtx, rule: Rule): boolean {
309	  const denom = rule.denom ?? 1;
310	  const num = rule.num ?? 1;
311	  if (rule.noLuck) {
312	    if (denom <= 1) return true;
313	    return ctx.rng.int(0, denom - 1) < num;
314	  }
315	  return rollLuck(ctx, denom, num);
316	}
317	
318	function pushDrop(out: NpcDropOutcome, id: number, n: number) {
319	  if (n > 0) out.drops.push({ id, n });
320	}
321	
322	function condPass(rule: Rule, ctx: NpcDropCtx): boolean | null {
323	  if (!rule.cond) return true;
324	  const pass = evalCond(rule.cond.name, rule.cond.args ?? [], ctx);
325	  return pass;
326	}
327	
328	function evalRule(rule: Rule, ctx: NpcDropCtx, out: NpcDropOutcome): State {
329	  let state: State;
330	  switch (rule.k) {
331	    case 'common': {
332	      if (tryRoll(ctx, rule)) {
333	        pushDrop(out, rule.item, ctx.rng.int(rule.min ?? 1, rule.max ?? 1));
334	        state = 'success';
335	      } else state = 'failedRoll';
336	      break;
337	    }
338	    case 'rerolls': {
339	      // CommonDropWithRerolls：rerolls+1 次掷骰任一成功（继承 RollLuck，无 noLuck 变体）
340	      let hit = false;
341	      for (let i = 0; i <= (rule.rerolls ?? 0); i++) hit = hit || rollLuck(ctx, rule.denom ?? 1, rule.num ?? 1);
342	      if (hit) { pushDrop(out, rule.item, ctx.rng.int(rule.min ?? 1, rule.max ?? 1)); state = 'success'; }
343	      else state = 'failedRoll';
344	      break;
345	    }
346	    case 'cond': {
347	      // ItemDropWithConditionRule：条件不过 = DoesntFillConditions（failedRoll 链不进）
348	      if (!condPass(rule, ctx)) { state = 'failedCond'; break; }
349	      if (rollLuck(ctx, rule.denom ?? 1, rule.num ?? 1)) {
350	        pushDrop(out, rule.item, ctx.rng.int(rule.min ?? 1, rule.max ?? 1));
351	        state = 'success';
352	      } else state = 'failedRoll';
353	      break;
354	    }
355	    case 'oneOf': {
356	      // OneFromOptionsDropRule：过掷后等权取一。提取器保证 ids 非空（空池会带
357	      // _unresolved 备案），防御性兜底防 ids.length-1 为负
358	      if (!rule.ids?.length) {
359	        if (rule._unresolved) warnOnce(`unresolved:${rule._unresolved}`, `oneOf 池未解析：${rule._unresolved}（跳过）`);
360	        state = 'failedRoll';
361	        break;
362	      }
363	      if (tryRoll(ctx, rule)) {
364	        pushDrop(out, rule.ids[ctx.rng.int(0, rule.ids.length - 1)], 1);
365	        state = 'success';
366	      } else state = 'failedRoll';
367	      break;
368	    }
369	    case 'expert': { // DropBasedOnExpertMode：normal/expert 二选一委托
370	      state = evalRule(ctx.isExpert ? rule.expert : rule.normal, ctx, out);
371	      break;
372	    }
373	    case 'master': { // DropBasedOnMasterMode
374	      state = evalRule(ctx.isMaster ? rule.master : rule.def, ctx, out);
375	      break;
376	    }
377	    case 'masterExpert': { // DropBasedOnMasterAndExpertMode：master>expert>def
378	      state = evalRule(ctx.isMaster ? rule.master : ctx.isExpert ? rule.expert : rule.def, ctx, out);
379	      break;
380	    }
381	    case 'bossBag': {
382	      // BossBag = DropBasedOnExpertMode(DropNothing, local(bag)+value=0)；
383	      // DropNothing 状态 = DoesntFillConditions（非专家分支不进 OnSuccess 链）
384	      if (rule.cond && !evalCond(rule.cond.name, rule.cond.args ?? [], ctx)) { state = 'failedCond'; break; }
385	      if (ctx.isExpert) {
386	        // local 规则 rng.Next(1)<1 恒过 → 掉袋 + 清 value
387	        pushDrop(out, rule.item, 1);
388	        out.zeroValue = true;
389	        state = 'success';
390	      } else state = 'failedCond';
391	      break;
392	    }
393	    case 'masterCommon': { // ByCondition(IsMasterMode, item)（非 master = DoesntFillConditions）
394	      if (ctx.isMaster) { pushDrop(out, rule.item, 1); state = 'success'; }
395	      else state = 'failedCond';
396	      break;
397	    }
398	    case 'masterAll': { // DropBasedOnMasterMode(DropNothing, perPlayer(item, denom, IsMasterMode))
399	      if (ctx.isMaster) {
400	        // DropPerPlayerOnThePlayer → CommonCode.DropItemForEachInteractingPlayerOnThePlayer
401	        // （CommonCode.cs:57-70）：掷骰 rng.Next(denom)<1（不走 Luck）；且无论掷中与否
402	        // 收尾 `npc.value = 0f`——即大师模式 Boss 掉宠物这条恒清钱币（曾漏：宠物+钱币双掉）
403	        out.zeroValue = true;
404	        if ((rule.denom ?? 1) > 1 && ctx.rng.int(0, (rule.denom ?? 1) - 1) < 1) {
405	          pushDrop(out, rule.item, 1);
406	        }
407	        state = 'success';
408	      } else state = 'failedCond';
409	      break;
410	    }
411	    case 'local': case 'perPlayer': {
412	      // DropLocalPerClientAndResetsNPCMoneyTo0：rng.Next 掷骰 + value 清零（单人化=直接掉）
413	      if (rule.cond && !evalCond(rule.cond.name, rule.cond.args ?? [], ctx)) { state = 'failedCond'; break; }
414	      if (ctx.rng.int(0, Math.max(1, rule.denom ?? 1) - 1) < 1) {
415	        pushDrop(out, rule.item, ctx.rng.int(rule.min ?? 1, rule.max ?? 1));
416	        out.zeroValue = true;
417	        state = 'success';
418	      } else state = 'failedRoll';
419	      break;
420	    }
421	    case 'gate': {
422	      // LeadingConditionRule：CanDrop=cond、DropAttempt 恒 success → 放行 OnSuccess 链
423	      state = condPass(rule, ctx) ? 'success' : 'failedCond';
424	      break;
425	    }
426	    case 'nothing': state = 'failedCond'; break; // DropNothing 状态 = DoesntFillConditions
427	    case 'mechSpawn': {
428	      // MechBossSpawnersDropRule：value>0 && hardMode && 未三王齐灭 → 各 1/2500
429	      const can = ctx.value > 0 && ctx.hardMode && !downed(ctx, 134, 125, 127);
430	      if (!can) { state = 'failedCond'; break; }
431	      if (!downed(ctx, 134) && rollLuck(ctx, 2500, 1)) { pushDrop(out, 556, 1); state = 'success'; break; }
432	      if (!downed(ctx, 125) && rollLuck(ctx, 2500, 1)) { pushDrop(out, 544, 1); state = 'success'; break; }
433	      if (!downed(ctx, 127) && rollLuck(ctx, 2500, 1)) { pushDrop(out, 557, 1); state = 'success'; break; }
434	      state = 'failedRoll';
435	      break;
436	    }
437	    case 'slimeBody': case 'statueMimic': {
438	      // SlimeBodyItemDropRule/StatueMimicItemDropRule：依赖 npc.ai[1]（雕像携带物品）
439	      // 我们无雕像产怪 → 恒不触发
440	      state = 'failedCond';
441	      break;
442	    }
443	    case 'dropOneByOne': {
444	      // DropOneByOne（DropOneByOne.cs TryDroppingItem 1:1）。注册仅四塔碎片
445	      // （ItemDropDatabase.cs RegisterBoss_LunarTowers :610-629），参数 json 未数值化、
446	      // 按 :611-627 硬编码：普通 12-20 块 × 每块 1-3；专家块数同、每块基数 ×1.5
447	      // （min=(int)1.5=1 / max=(int)4.5=4）+ 每活跃玩家 +1/块。
448	      // ChanceNumerator/Denominator=1 → RollLuck(1) 恒过
449	      {
450	        const expert = ctx.isExpert;
451	        const chunks = ctx.rng.int(12, 20);                    // Next(12, 21)
452	        const players = 1;                                     // Main.CurrentFrameFlags.ActivePlayersCount（单人）
453	        const minStack = (expert ? 1 : 1) + players * (expert ? 1 : 0);
454	        const maxStack = (expert ? 4 : 3) + players * (expert ? 1 : 0);
455	        for (let i = 0; i < chunks; i++) {
456	          pushDrop(out, rule.item, ctx.rng.int(minStack, maxStack));
457	        }
458	        state = 'success';
459	      }
460	      break;
461	    }
462	    case 'oneOfRules': { // OneFromRulesRule：rng.Next(denom) 过后等权取一，
463	      // 被选中的 option 走完整子求值（含其自身链）；规则状态恒以掷骰为准
464	      // （OneFromRulesRule.cs TryDroppingItem(info, resolveAction)）
465	      if (rollLuck(ctx, rule.denom ?? 1, 1)) {
466	        evalRule(rule.rules[ctx.rng.int(0, rule.rules.length - 1)], ctx, out);
467	        state = 'success';
468	      } else state = 'failedRoll';
469	      break;
470	    }
471	    case 'listRef': { // List<IItemDropRule>{…}（OneFromRulesRule 参数）
472	      state = 'success';
473	      break;
474	    }
475	    case 'noRepeat': { // FromOptionsWithoutRepeatsDropRule.cs:27-45：
476	      // CanDrop 恒 true；dropCount 次不放回抽样（每件 rng.Next(len) 取一后移除），
477	      // 每件数量恒 1；规则状态恒 Success（无掷骰）
478	      {
479	        const pool = [...(rule.ids as number[])];
480	        for (let i = 0; i < (rule.count ?? 0); i++) {
481	          if (pool.length <= 0) break;
482	          const idx = ctx.rng.int(0, pool.length - 1);
483	          pushDrop(out, pool[idx], 1);
484	          pool.splice(idx, 1);
485	        }
486	        state = 'success';
487	      }
488	      break;
489	    }
490	    default:
491	      // 未知 kind：按 DoesntFillConditions 处理（曾返回 success——会让挂在它身上的
492	      // OnSuccess 链整支误触发；failedCond 则三条链全部不进，语义最保守）
493	      warnOnce(`rule:${rule.k}`, `未实现的规则 kind ${rule.k}（按 failedCond 跳过）`);
494	      state = 'failedCond';
495	      break;
496	  }
497	  // 链：按父状态门控（Chains.cs TryIfSucceeded/TryIfFailedRandomRoll/TryIfDoesntFillConditions）
498	  for (const c of rule.chain ?? []) {
499	    if ((c.on === 'success' && state === 'success')
500	      || (c.on === 'failedRoll' && state === 'failedRoll')
501	      || (c.on === 'failedConditions' && state === 'failedCond')) {
502	      evalRule(c.rule, ctx, out);
503	    }
504	  }
505	  return state;
506	}
507	
508	/**
509	 * 规则掉落主入口（ItemDropSolver.TryDropping 语义）：
510	 * 全局规则 + 该 netID（回退 type）规则按注册序独立求值。
511	 */
512	export function resolveDrops(ctx: NpcDropCtx): NpcDropOutcome {
513	  const out: NpcDropOutcome = { drops: [], zeroValue: false };
514	  const rules = [
515	    ...globalRules,
516	    ...(rulesByNpc.get(ctx.netId) ?? rulesByNpc.get(ctx.type) ?? []),
517	  ];
518	  for (const r of rules) evalRule(r, ctx, out);
519	  return out;
520	}
521	
522	// ============ 钱币管线（NPCLoot_DropMoney :80412 1:1） ============
523	export function rollCoins(value: number, ctx: NpcDropCtx, bloodMoon: boolean, midas = false): ResolvedDrop[] {
524	  // :80414-80417：|luck| 概率掷两轮；:80477-80487 第一轮存底，第二轮 luck>0 取大 /
525	  // luck<0 取小（幸运取极值——曾两个分支都是单掷，重掷门形同虚设）
526	  const luck = ctx.luck ?? 0;
527	  const twice = luck !== 0 && ctx.rng.next() < Math.abs(luck);
528	  let num = coinAmountOnce(value, ctx, bloodMoon, midas);
529	  if (twice) {
530	    const second = coinAmountOnce(value, ctx, bloodMoon, midas);
531	    num = luck > 0 ? Math.max(num, second) : Math.min(num, second);
532	  }
533	  return splitCoins(num, ctx.rng);
534	}
535	/** 单轮金额（:80422-80459 的乘子链，抽签顺序照抄：midas 在基线掷之前） */
536	function coinAmountOnce(value: number, ctx: NpcDropCtx, bloodMoon: boolean, midas: boolean): number {
537	  const rng = ctx.rng;
538	  let num = value;
539	  if (midas) num *= 1 + rng.int(10, 50) * 0.01;   // 金药剂 midas（:80422 Next(10,51) 左闭右开 → int(10,50)；rng.int(a,b)=Next(a,b+1) 约定）
540	  num *= 1 + rng.int(-20, 75) * 0.01;             // :80425 Next(-20,76) → [-20,75]
541	  if (rng.int(0, 1) === 0) num *= 1 + rng.int(5, 10) * 0.01;    // 1/2 ×(1.05-1.10)
542	  if (rng.int(0, 3) === 0) num *= 1 + rng.int(10, 20) * 0.01;   // 1/4 ×(1.10-1.20)
543	  if (rng.int(0, 7) === 0) num *= 1 + rng.int(15, 30) * 0.01;   // 1/8 ×(1.15-1.30)
544	  if (rng.int(0, 15) === 0) num *= 1 + rng.int(20, 40) * 0.01;  // 1/16 ×(1.20-1.40)
545	  if (rng.int(0, 31) === 0) num *= 1 + rng.int(25, 50) * 0.01;  // 1/32 ×(1.25-1.50)
546	  if (rng.int(0, 63) === 0) num *= 1 + rng.int(50, 100) * 0.01; // 1/64 ×(1.50-2.00)
547	  if (bloodMoon) num *= 1 + rng.int(0, 100) * 0.01;             // :80457 Next(101)
548	  return num;
549	}
550	/** 贪心拆币（:80489-80540，大额先裂变再按面额摊） */
551	function splitCoins(num: number, rng: RNG): ResolvedDrop[] {
552	  const drops: ResolvedDrop[] = [];
553	  while (Math.floor(num) > 0) {
554	    if (num > 1000000) {
555	      let n = Math.floor(num / 1000000);
556	      if (n > 50 && rng.int(0, 4) === 0) n = Math.floor(n / (rng.int(0, 2) + 1));
557	      if (rng.int(0, 4) === 0) n = Math.floor(n / (rng.int(0, 2) + 1));
558	      let rest = n;
559	      while (rest > 999) { rest -= 999; drops.push({ id: 74, n: 999 }); }
560	      num -= 1000000 * n;
561	      if (rest > 0) drops.push({ id: 74, n: rest });
562	    } else if (num > 10000) {
563	      let n = Math.floor(num / 10000);
564	      if (n > 50 && rng.int(0, 4) === 0) n = Math.floor(n / (rng.int(0, 2) + 1));
565	      if (rng.int(0, 4) === 0) n = Math.floor(n / (rng.int(0, 2) + 1));
566	      num -= 10000 * n;
567	      if (n > 0) drops.push({ id: 73, n });
568	    } else if (num > 100) {
569	      let n = Math.floor(num / 100);
570	      if (n > 50 && rng.int(0, 4) === 0) n = Math.floor(n / (rng.int(0, 2) + 1));
571	      if (rng.int(0, 4) === 0) n = Math.floor(n / (rng.int(0, 2) + 1));
572	      num -= 100 * n;
573	      if (n > 0) drops.push({ id: 72, n });
574	    } else {
575	      let n = Math.floor(num);
576	      if (n > 50 && rng.int(0, 4) === 0) n = Math.floor(n / (rng.int(0, 2) + 1));
577	      if (rng.int(0, 4) === 0) n = Math.floor(n / (rng.int(0, 3) + 1));
578	      n = Math.max(1, n);
579	      num -= n;
580	      drops.push({ id: 71, n });
581	    }
582	  }
583	  return drops;
584	}
585	
586	// ============ 心/星（NPCLoot_DropCommonLifeAndMana :80332 1:1） ============
587	export function rollHeartsAndStars(ctx: NpcDropCtx): ResolvedDrop[] {
588	  const drops: ResolvedDrop[] = [];
589	  if (ctx.type !== 16 && ctx.type !== 81 && ctx.type !== 121
590	    && ctx.lifeMax > 1 && ctx.damage > 0) {
591	    if (ctx.rng.int(0, 5) === 0) { // RollLuck(6)==0
592	      if (ctx.rng.int(0, 1) === 0 && ctx.mana < ctx.manaMax) drops.push({ id: 184, n: 1 });
593	      else if (ctx.rng.int(0, 1) === 0 && ctx.hp < ctx.hpMax) drops.push({ id: 58, n: 1 });
594	    }
595	    if (ctx.rng.int(0, 1) === 0 && ctx.mana < ctx.manaMax) drops.push({ id: 184, n: 1 }); // RollLuck(2)==0
596	  }
597	  return drops;
598	}
599	
600	// ============ Boss 药水+心（DoDeathEvents_DropBossPotionsAndHearts :79790） ============
601	export function rollBossPotionsAndHearts(ctx: NpcDropCtx): ResolvedDrop[] {
602	  const drops: ResolvedDrop[] = [];
603	  const stack = ctx.rng.int(5, 15); // Next(5,16)
604	  let potion = 28; // 弱效治疗药水
605	  if (ctx.type === 113 || ctx.type === 35 || ctx.type === 668) potion = 188; // 治疗药水
606	  else if (ctx.type === 222) potion = 1134;
607	  else if (ctx.type === 657) potion = 499;
608	  else if (ctx.type > 113 && ctx.type < 222) potion = 499; // 强效治疗
609	  else if (ctx.type === 636 || ctx.type === 245 || ctx.type === 262 || ctx.type === 370
610	    || ctx.type === 395 || ctx.type === 439) potion = 499;
611	  else if (ctx.type === 398) potion = 3544;
612	  drops.push({ id: potion, n: stack });
613	  const hearts = ctx.rng.int(5, 9); // Next(5)+5 → 5..9? 原版 Next(5)+5 = 5..9
614	  for (let i = 0; i < hearts; i++) drops.push({ id: 58, n: 1 });
615	  return drops;
616	}
617	
618	// ============ 掉落初速度（Item.cs:49325-49337） ============
619	export function dropVelocity(rng: RNG): { vx: number; vy: number } {
620	  return { vx: rng.int(-30, 30) * 0.1, vy: rng.int(-40, -15) * 0.1 };
621	}
622	
623	export { SLIME_BODY };
624
```

```
// 物品注册表
export type ToolType = 'pick' | 'axe' | 'sword' | 'hammer';
// Buff 类型（药水饮用后生效）
export type PotionBuff = 'agility' | 'ironskin' | 'resistance' | 'thorns' | 'regen'
  | 'obsidianSkin' | 'gills' | 'manaRegen' | 'magicPower' | 'featherfall' | 'waterWalking' | 'archery' | 'nightOwl';
// 盔甲部位：0 头盔 1 胸甲 2 胫甲（与 Inventory.armorSlotReq 的格位对应）
export type ArmorSlot = 0 | 1 | 2;

export interface ItemDef {
  key: string;
  name: string;
  maxStack: number;
  tile?: string;          // 放置的 tile key
  placeStyle?: number;     // 放置的样式索引(vanilla style 帧 X 偏移 = style*fw*18)
  magic?: {               // 魔法武器:消耗 mana 发射投射物
    damage: number;
    speed: number;        // 使用冷却 tick
    manaCost: number;
    projSpeed: number;
    proj?: 'spark' | 'bolt'; // 投射物外观
  };
  ranged?: {              // 远程武器:消耗 ammo 弹药发射投射物
    damage: number;
    speed: number;        // 使用冷却 tick
    knockback: number;
    ammo: string;         // 弹药 item key(箭)
    projSpeed: number;    // 投射物速度 px/tick
    /** 原版 item id（PickAmmo/ItemCheck_Shoot 数值以 vanilla-itemcombat.json 为准） */
    vid?: number;
  };
  /** 原版 AmmoID（40=箭；弹药物品自身标识，PickAmmo 匹配弓 useAmmo） */
  ammoId?: number;
  /** 原版 item id（战斗数值数据源） */
  vid?: number;
  wall?: string;          // 放置的 wall key
  /** 原版 createWall（Item.cs SetDefaults）：放置的背景墙 vanilla id。
   *  vanilla-wallitems.json 全量 124 项（tools/extract-wallitems.mjs 提取） */
  wallId?: number;
  tool?: {
    type: ToolType;
    power?: number;       // 工具力（镐/斧/锤）
    damage?: number;      // 武器伤害
    speed?: number;       // 挥砍动画 tick（≈原版 useAnimation）
    /** 原版 useTime（Item.cs SetDefaults）：挖掘每击冷却——原版挖掘节奏的真基准，
     *  铜 15-23 / 铁 13-20 / 银 11-19 / 金 17-23（applyItemTime 冷却=useTime） */
    useTime?: number;
    knockback?: number;
    reach?: number;       // 攻击/作用半径（px）
  };
  /** 副工具力（Hamaxe 系双工具：主 tool.type 外的另一力，挖掘入口按主类型判定） */
  axePower?: number;
  heal?: number;          // 食用/饮用回复
  potion?: {
    buff: PotionBuff;
    duration: number;     // 秒
    isHealType: boolean;  // 受"耐药性"封锁
  };
  accessory?: 'lucky_horseshoe' | 'feral_claws'; // 配饰效果
  armor?: { slot: ArmorSlot; defense: number };   // 盔甲（可穿装备/时装格，仅装备格计防御）
  value?: number;         // 钱币价值（铜币）
===GREP ItemDef===
```

```
170	/* Loadout 三色（ItemSlot.LoadoutSlotColors :322-340，×inventoryBack 灰 = multiply）：
171	 * 切换瞬间白闪（GetColorByLoadout :2546：Remap(dt,0,30,0.5,0)³ lerp→白） */
172	@keyframes sw-loadout-flash { 0% { background:rgb(255 255 255 / 0.5); }
173	  100% { background:rgb(255 255 255 / 0); } }
174	.sw-loadout-flash .sw-slot::before { animation:sw-loadout-flash 0.5s ease-out; }
175	/* 物品转移 hue 脉冲（ItemSlot.cs :2144-2152：贴图换 Back13 +
176	 *  Lerp(基色(63,65,151)×invAlpha, hslToRgb(hue,1,0.5), (time/300)²/2)——
177	 *  CSS 近似：色层 alpha 沿二次曲线 0.5→0 衰减，5s（300 tick） */
178	@keyframes sw-slot-glow { 0% { opacity:0.5; } 100% { opacity:0; } }
179	.sw-slot .sw-glow-overlay { position:absolute; inset:0; pointer-events:none;

181	/* 金币/弹药槽 0.6 缩放（Main.cs:41193/41211 inventoryScale=0.6 → 31px、列距 33.6） */
182	.sw-slot.sm { width:31px; height:31px; }

184	.sw-slot.sm .cnt { font-size:10px; left:2px; }

192	.sw-hotbar .sw-slot:hover { filter:none; }
```

```
2321	          dctx.drawImage(shield.img, shield.sx + col * 52, shield.sy + 48, 52, 48, 0, 0, 52, 48);
2322	          dctx.globalAlpha = 1;
2323	        }
2324	      }
2325	      defEl.querySelector('span')!.textContent = String(p.defense);
2326	    }
2327	    const stationsEl = this.invPanel.querySelector('[data-role="stations"]') as HTMLElement;
2328	    if (stationsEl) {
2329	      const stName: Record<string, string> = { hand: Lang.text('Mods.SandboxWorld.UI.StationHand'), workbench: '🛠 ' + Lang.text('ItemName.WorkBench'), furnace: '🔥 ' + Lang.text('ItemName.Furnace'), anvil: '⚒ ' + Lang.text('ItemName.IronAnvil') };
2330	      stationsEl.textContent = Lang.text('Mods.SandboxWorld.UI.CraftStations', [...stations].map((s) => stName[s] ?? s).join('、'));
2331	    }
2332	    this.craftListEl.innerHTML = '';
2333	    // 自制 RECIPES 表已随本地物品退役整体移除——合成列表只留原版配方段
2334	    // （3173 条，Recipe.UpdateRecipeList 主干：环境+材料双门）
2335	    this.refreshVanillaCrafting();
2336	  }
2337	
2338	  /** 鱼眼合成列表焦点行（原版 focusRecipe；滚轮 ±1，CraftingUI 滚动 ±6.5/帧） */
2339	  private craftFocus = 0;
2340	  /** Modern 合成网格：搜索栏 + 焦点配方材料格（1.4.5 CraftingGrid 语义） */
2341	  private craftSearchEl: HTMLInputElement | null = null;
2342	  private craftGridEl: HTMLElement | null = null;
2343	  /** 材料格已存（原版 vid → 数量；焦点配方切换/关面板时归还背包） */
2344	  private craftGrid = new Map<number, number>();
2345	  private craftGridRecipe: VanillaRecipeLike | null = null;
2346	  /** 原版配方列表（数据驱动 1456 全量；鱼眼缩放 = 100/(|Δ|+100) 下限 0.75，CraftingUI.cs:184-192 DOM 近似） */
2347	  private refreshVanillaCrafting(): void {
2348	    const game = this.game;
2349	    if (!game || !this.craftListEl) return;
2350	    let avail = game.vanillaAvailableRecipes();
2351	    if (!avail.length) return;
2352	    // 搜索过滤（Modern 网格搜索栏：按成品名过滤）
2353	    const q = this.craftSearchEl?.value.trim().toLowerCase() ?? '';
2354	    if (q) {
2355	      const filtered = avail.filter(({ r }) => {
2356	        const k = vanillaItemKey(r.create);
2357	        const name = k ? Lang.itemNameByKey(k) : '';
2358	        return name.toLowerCase().includes(q);
2359	      });
2360	      if (filtered.length) avail = filtered;
2361	    }
2362	    const head = el('div', 'sw-lbl', '── 原版配方 ──');
2363	    this.craftListEl.appendChild(head);
2364	    const MAX_ROWS = 60; // 焦点 ±30（超出原版 middleY 视口即 alpha→0 :177）
2365	    const start = Math.max(0, Math.min(this.craftFocus - 30, avail.length - MAX_ROWS));
2366	    if (this.craftFocus >= avail.length) this.craftFocus = 0;
2367	    for (let idx = start; idx < Math.min(avail.length, start + MAX_ROWS); idx++) {
2368	      const { r, count } = avail[idx];
2369	      const dy = idx - this.craftFocus; // 相对焦点的连续偏移（原版 availableRecipeY）
2370	      const scale = Math.max(0.75, 100 / (Math.abs(dy) + 100));
2371	      const outKey = vanillaItemKey(r.create);
2372	      if (!outKey) continue;
2373	      const row = el('div', 'sw-recipe');
2374	      row.style.cssText += ` transform:scale(${scale.toFixed(3)}); transform-origin:left center; opacity:${(1 - Math.min(0.75, Math.abs(dy) / 30)).toFixed(2)}; transition:transform 0.08s;`;
2375	      const img = el('img') as HTMLImageElement;
2376	      img.src = iconUrl(game, game.itemId(outKey));
2377	      row.appendChild(img);
2378	      const label = el('span', '', `${Lang.itemNameByKey(outKey)}${r.createStack > 1 ? ` ×${r.createStack}` : ''} ×${count}`);
2379	      row.appendChild(label);
2380	      const mats = el('span', '', r.items.map((req) => {
2381	        const k = ingredientKey(req.id);
2382	        if (!k) return ` ? ${req.stack}`;
2383	        const have = game.player.inv.countOf(game.itemId(k));
2384	        return ` ${Lang.itemNameByKey(k)} ${Math.min(have, req.stack)}/${req.stack}`;
2385	      }).join(' ·'));
2386	      mats.style.cssText = 'margin-left:auto; color:#9a8ab8; font-size:11px; text-align:right;';
2387	      row.appendChild(mats);
2388	      row.addEventListener('mousedown', (ev) => {
2389	        ev.stopPropagation();
2390	        this.craftFocus = idx; // 选中置焦（原版 focusRecipe=i）
2391	        game.vanillaCraft(r, (ev as MouseEvent).shiftKey ? 10 : 1);
2392	        this.refreshAll();
2393	      });
2394	      this.craftListEl.appendChild(row);
2395	    }
2396	    // 滚轮：焦点 ±1（原版 ScrollCraftingList；±6.5/帧的平滑由 transition 兜）
2397	    if (!this._craftWheelBound) {
2398	      this._craftWheelBound = true;
2399	      this.craftListEl.addEventListener('wheel', (ev) => {
2400	        ev.preventDefault();
2401	        ev.stopPropagation();
2402	        this.craftFocus = Math.max(0, this.craftFocus + (ev.deltaY > 0 ? 1 : -1));
2403	        this.refreshAll();
2404	      }, { passive: false });
2405	    }
2406	    // Modern 合成网格材料格（焦点配方）
2407	    this.refreshCraftGrid(avail);
2408	  }
2409	  private _craftWheelBound = false;
2410	
2411	  /** 材料格覆盖计数：req（含组假 id）当前格内存量 */
2412	  private gridCovered(reqId: number): number {
2413	    if (reqId < 1000000) return this.craftGrid.get(reqId) ?? 0;
2414	    const items = RECIPE_GROUP_ITEMS[String(reqId - 1000000)] ?? [];
2415	    let n = 0;
2416	    for (const m of items) n += this.craftGrid.get(m) ?? 0;
2417	    return n;
2418	  }
2419	

```

```
1	// 盔甲/配饰基础属性运行时（tools/extract-equip-prefix.mjs → vanilla-itemstats.json）。
2	// vi_ 批次物品注册期无 armor/accessory 字段——槽位归属与防御值以本表为权威：
3	//   s: 'h'|'b'|'l' 盔甲部位（headSlot/bodySlot/legSlot 赋值即归属，Item.cs SetDefaults）
4	//   def: 防御值（defense = N 字面量）
5	//   acc: 1 功能配饰 / 0 社交配饰（vanity=true；无功能效果）
6	import statsJson from './vanilla-itemstats.json';
7	import { ITEM_DEFS } from './items';
8	
9	export interface VanillaItemStat {
10	  s?: 'h' | 'b' | 'l';
11	  def?: number;
12	  acc?: 0 | 1;
13	  life?: number;   // 生命回复（Item.lifeRegen，再生手环族）
14	  wing?: number;   // 翅膀槽（Item.wingSlot>0 即翅膀；飞行属性查 vanilla-wingstats）
15	  hs?: number; bs?: number; ls?: number; // 盔甲绘制槽序号（套装匹配键，Player.cs UpdateArmorSets）
16	
17	  str?: number;    // 悠悠球线（Item.stringColor>0 → yoyoString，Player.cs:14170）
18	}
19	
20	const STATS = statsJson as Record<string, VanillaItemStat>;
21	
22	/** 原版 item id → 盔甲/配饰属性 */
23	export function vanillaStatOfVid(vid: number): VanillaItemStat | null {
24	  return STATS[String(vid)] ?? null;
25	}
26	
27	/** 内部 item id → 属性（ITEM_DEFS 缓存） */
28	const STAT_BY_IID = (() => {
29	  const m = new Map<number, VanillaItemStat>();
30	  for (let i = 0; i < ITEM_DEFS.length; i++) {
31	    const d = ITEM_DEFS[i];
32	    if (!d) continue;
33	    const vid = d.vid ?? (d.key.startsWith('vi_') ? parseInt(d.key.slice(3), 10) : -1);
34	    if (vid < 0) continue;
35	    const s = STATS[String(vid)];
36	    if (s) m.set(i, s);
37	  }
38	  return m;
39	})();
40	
41	export function statOfInternal(id: number): VanillaItemStat | null {
42	  return STAT_BY_IID.get(id) ?? null;
43	}
44	
45	/** 盔甲部位槽序（0 头 / 1 身 / 2 腿；非盔甲 -1） */
46	export function armorSlotIndexOfInternal(id: number): number {
47	  const s = STAT_BY_IID.get(id)?.s;
48	  return s === 'h' ? 0 : s === 'b' ? 1 : s === 'l' ? 2 : -1;
49	}
50	
51	/** 功能配饰判定（ItemSlot context 10：accessory && !vanity） */
52	export function isFunctionalAccessory(id: number): boolean {
53	  return STAT_BY_IID.get(id)?.acc === 1;
54	}
55	
56	/** 任意配饰（含社交）判定 */
57	export function isAnyAccessory(id: number): boolean {
58	  const acc = STAT_BY_IID.get(id)?.acc;
59	  return acc !== undefined;
60	}
61
```

```
1499	  /** 材料的配方列表（CollectGuideRecipes：items 直接命中或 RecipeGroup 成员命中） */
1500	  private renderGuideRecipes(listEl: HTMLElement, vid: number): void {
1501	    listEl.textContent = '';
1502	    const game = this.game;
1503	    const avail = new Map<unknown, number>();
1504	    for (const { r, count } of game.vanillaAvailableRecipes()) avail.set(r, count);
1505	    let hits = 0;
1506	    for (const r of VANILLA_RECIPES) {
1507	      const used = r.items.some((req) => req.id === vid
1508	        || (req.id >= 1000000 && (RECIPE_GROUP_ITEMS[String(req.id - 1000000)] ?? []).includes(vid)));
1509	      if (!used) continue;
1510	      if (++hits > 60) { listEl.appendChild(el('div', 'sw-lbl', '…更多结果请细化')); break; }
1511	      const outKey = vanillaItemKey(r.create);
1512	      if (!outKey) continue;
1513	      const row = el('div', 'sw-recipe' + (avail.has(r) ? '' : ' no'));
1514	      const img = el('img') as HTMLImageElement;
1515	      img.src = iconUrl(game, game.itemId(outKey));
1516	      row.appendChild(img);
1517	      const label = el('span', '', `${Lang.itemNameByKey(outKey)}${r.createStack > 1 ? ` ×${r.createStack}` : ''}`);
1518	      row.appendChild(label);
1519	      const info = el('span', '', `${r.tile >= 0 ? (Lang.tileName(r.tile) ?? `站点 ${r.tile}`) : Lang.text('Mods.SandboxWorld.UI.StationHand')} · ${
1520	        r.items.map((req) => `${req.id >= 1000000 ? '任何组物' : (Lang.itemNameByKey(ingredientKey(req.id) ?? '') ?? req.id)} ${req.stack}`).join(' +')}`);
1521	      info.style.cssText = 'margin-left:auto; color:#9a8ab8; font-size:11px; text-align:right; max-width:280px;';
1522	      row.appendChild(info);
1523	      if (avail.has(r)) {
1524	        row.onclick = () => {
1525	          game.vanillaCraft(r, 1);
1526	          this.refreshAll();
1527	        };
1528	      }
1529	      listEl.appendChild(row);
1530	    }
1531	    if (!hits) listEl.appendChild(el('div', 'sw-lbl', '该物品不用于任何配方'));
1532	  }

1555	      if (this.heldStack.stack <= 0) this.heldStack = null;
1556	    } else {
1557	      this.game.player.inv.trash = this.heldStack; // 交换：可把误放的东西取回
1558	      this.heldStack = t;
1559	    }
1560	    this.paintTrash();
1561	  }
```

```
// 镜像 terraria.wiki.gg 中文站: 枚举指定命名空间全部页面, 抓取 wikitext 存本地
// 用法: node wiki-mirror.mjs           # 抓正文(可重复运行, 自动续传)
//       node wiki-mirror.mjs --images  # 抓文件命名空间的图片本体
import fs from 'fs';
import path from 'path';
import { execSync } from 'child_process';

const API = 'https://terraria.wiki.gg/zh/api.php';
const OUT = path.resolve(path.dirname(new URL(import.meta.url).pathname), '../terraria-wiki-zh');
const PAGES = path.join(OUT, 'pages');
const INDEX = path.join(OUT, 'index.json');
const NS_DIRS = { 0: '主', 4: '项目', 6: '文件', 10: '模板', 12: '帮助', 14: '分类', 828: '模块', 10000: '指南', 11000: '旧版' };
const NSES = Object.keys(NS_DIRS).map(Number);
const sleep = ms => new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, ms));
const log = (...a) => fs.writeSync(2, a.join(' ') + '\n');

const UA = 'SandboxWorld-wiki-mirror/1.0 (personal offline reference; contact: local-script)';
const JAR = '/tmp/wiki-mirror-cookies.txt';

function curl(url, post) {
  let lastErr;
  for (let i = 0; i < 8; i++) {
    try {
      const cmd = post
        ? `curl -sfL --max-time 90 -A "${UA}" -b ${JAR} -c ${JAR} -X POST -d "${post.replace(/"/g, '\\"').replace(/\n/g, '')}" "${url}"`
        : `curl -sfL --max-time 90 -A "${UA}" -b ${JAR} -c ${JAR} "${url.replace(/"/g, '\\"')}"`;
      return execSync(cmd, { maxBuffer: 1024 * 1024 * 1024 }).toString('utf8');
    } catch (e) {
      lastErr = e;
      // 429/5xx/56: 指数退避
      const wait = Math.min(10 * 2 ** i, 120);
      execSync(`sleep ${wait}`);
    }
  }
  throw lastErr;
}
const apiGet = params => JSON.parse(curl(`${API}?format=json&formatversion=2&${params}`));
const apiPost = (params, body) => JSON.parse(curl(`${API}?format=json&formatversion=2&${params}`, body));

// ---------- 索引与存储 ----------
let index = { pages: {}, images: {} };
if (fs.existsSync(INDEX)) index = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(INDEX, 'utf8'));
const saveIndex = () => fs.writeFileSync(INDEX, JSON.stringify(index));

function pagePath(ns, title) {
  const dir = NS_DIRS[ns] ?? `ns${ns}`;
  // 去掉命名空间前缀, 保留子页路径, 清理非法字符
  let rel = title.includes(':') && ns !== 0 ? title.slice(title.indexOf(':') + 1) : title;
  rel = rel.replace(/[<>:"|?*\\]/g, '_').replace(/^\.+$/, '_');
  return path.join(PAGES, dir, ...rel.split('/')) + '.wiki';
}

// ---------- 枚举 ----------
async function listAll(ns) {
  const titles = [];
  let cont = '';
  for (;;) {
    const j = apiGet(`action=query&list=allpages&apnamespace=${ns}&aplimit=200${cont}`);
    for (const p of j.query.allpages) titles.push(p.title);
    if (!j.continue?.apcontinue) break;

#!/usr/bin/env python3
```

```
// 原版 NPC 对话头像（NPCID.Sets.NPCPortraits → Images/TownNPCs/Portraits/Portrait_<Name>，
// NPCID.cs:214 字典逐条提取；素材已复制到 public/sprites/portraits/）。
// Shimmer 变体按原版条件优先级省略（无微光态），取默认头像。
export const NPC_PORTRAITS: Record<number, string> = {
  17: 'Merchant', 18: 'Nurse', 19: 'ArmsDealer', 20: 'Dryad', 22: 'Guide',
  37: 'OldMan', 38: 'Demolitionist', 54: 'Clothier', 107: 'GoblinTinkerer',
  108: 'Wizard', 124: 'Mechanic', 142: 'Santa', 160: 'Truffle', 178: 'Steampunker',
  207: 'DyeTrader', 208: 'PartyGirl', 209: 'Cyborg', 227: 'Painter',
  228: 'WitchDoctor', 229: 'Pirate', 353: 'Stylist', 368: 'TravellingMerchant',
  369: 'Angler', 441: 'TaxCollector', 453: 'SkeletonMerchant', 550: 'Tavernkeep',
  588: 'Golfer', 663: 'Princess',
};

/** 头像 URL（无头像 NPC 返回 null——原版该字典不含全部类型） */
export function portraitUrl(vanillaId: number): string | null {
  const name = NPC_PORTRAITS[vanillaId];
  return name ? `sprites/portraits/Portrait_${name}.png` : null;
}
```

````
# In-Game Wiki/Guide Infrastructure Inventory — SandboxWorld `game/`

**Executive summary:** far more exists than expected. There is already a full bestiary UI with drop tables + spawn conditions, a working "what can I craft from item X" reverse-recipe panel (`openGuideSearch`), 3173 vanilla recipes with a clean query engine, 690+ NPC stat records, 2664 item-description strings sitting unused in l10n, and a 655MB offline Terraria wiki markdown corpus on disk. The missing pieces are the *reverse* indexes (recipes producing X, NPCs dropping X) and any join/aggregator across acquisition sources.

---

## 1. Bestiary UI — `game/src/ui/BestiaryPanel.ts` (988 lines, DOM-based)

**Framework:** plain DOM overlay with its own `sw-bst-*` CSS block (`BestiaryPanel.ts:473-531`), injected as a `<style id="sw-bst-style">` (`:568-573`). It only imports `UISfx` from the vui tree (`:30`) — **not** a vui/Canvas panel.

**What it shows** — full vanilla UIBestiaryTest replica:
- Grid of 72×72 entry cells, paged, `{first}-{last} ({total})` range text (`BST_CELL` `:44`, `bestiaryGridSize` `:46`, `bestiaryRangeText` `:53`)
- 9 sort modes (unlocks/id/bestiaryId/alpha/rarity/attack/defense/coins/hp) `:71-74`, `sortBestiaryRows` `:184-205`
- Filter chips: kind `all|enemy|town|critter`, boss, unlocked yes/no (`buildChips` `:647-662`, `filterBestiaryRows` `:208-219`) — **categories exist**, matching your "categories?" question
- Search box `:583`, wired at `:611` (filters unlocked entries by display name)
- Bottom completion bar `:806-813`
- **Detail pane** `renderInfo()` `:816-967`: name/tags (boss/critter/gold/locked), **出没环境 spawn-condition tags** `:846-863`, large portrait with MapBG-derived background `drawPortrait` `:724-788`, kill count, **flavor text** `:876-887`, rarity stars `:892-896`, stats (Life/Attack/Defense/Knockback) `:898-905`, and a **full drop table with item icons + drop rates + conditions** `:908-951` (capped at 30 rows).

**Key reusable logic (pure, testable — DOM is just a consumer):**
- `flattenDropsForNpc(npcId): DropLine[]` `:312-326` → `{item, chance|null, min, max, cond, tag}` — this **is** the "某怪掉什么" query, already built, deduped, rate-sorted. `walkDrop` `:251-309` handles expert/master/noRepeat/oneOfRules/mechSpawn/gate chains.
- `bestiarySpawnConditions(npcId)` `:377-385`, `bestiaryFlavorText(npcId)` `:399-421`, `bestiaryPortraitBackground` `:447`, `bestiaryGridBackground` `:463`
- `bestiaryRows(tracker)` `:156`, `bestiaryRepNpcId(creditId)` `:114`, `npcDisplayName` `:176`

**Data source** — `game/src/data/Bestiary.ts` (data layer, 1:1 port of `Terraria.GameContent.Bestiary`):
- `bestiaryEntries()` `:131` — ~546 entries, kinds enemy/town/critter, kill/sight/chat sources, gold critter gates
- `UnlockState` enum `:87-93` (NotKnown/PortraitOnly/Stats/DropsNoRates/DropsWithRates) — the gating system
- `BestiaryTracker` class `:202` — kills/sights/chats maps, `unlockState()` `:232`, `completion()` `:255`, save/restore
- `bestiaryRarityStars()` `:338`, `bestiarySortingId()` `:381` (9-key orderby chain)
- Statics from `vanilla-bestiary.json` (`townNpcBestiaryPriority` 41, `normalGoldCritterBestiaryPriority` 47, `bossBestiaryPriority` 62, `rarityStarOverrides` 137, `npcStats` 761 tuples `[rarity,boss,damage,defense,lifeMax,aiStyle]`) imported at `:307-320`, plus `bestiaryStatics.generated.ts` (HIDE/REDIRECT/KILLS_TO_FULL)

**How it's opened:** `game/src/mainFlow.ts:630-646` `openBestiary()` — saves pause/uiBlocking state, sets `g.paused = true; g.input.uiBlocking = true`, constructs `new BestiaryPanel(root, {...})`, restores on `onBack`. Entry points: `UI.ts:275` (`onBestiary` hook), inventory button `UI.ts:1663-1669` ('图鉴'), pause menu button `UI.ts:2615`. Esc/KeyE close via capture-phase window handler `BestiaryPanel.ts:552-558, :618` to avoid main.ts double-consumption.

## 2. Recipe data

**`game/src/data/vanilla-recipes.json`** — **3173 recipes** (field `count: 3173`). Top-level: `{count, groups(31), groupItems(31), tileCountsAs(9), recipes[3173]}`.

Schema (`vanillaRecipes.ts:10-24`):
```ts
{ create: number; createStack: number; tile: number; // -1 = by hand
  items: Array<{id: number; stack: number}>; groups: number[];
  honey?/water?/lava?/snow?/graveyard?/alchemy?, notDecraftable?, crimson?, corruption?,
  shimmer?: Array<[number,number]> }
```
Group fake-ids are `1000000 + groupIndex`; `groups` on a recipe lists the group indices it accepts. Sample: `{"create":8,"createStack":3,"tile":-1,"items":[{"id":23,"stack":1},{"id":9,"stack":1}],"groups":[24]}`.

**Engine API — `game/src/data/vanillaRecipes.ts`:**
- Exports `VANILLA_RECIPES`, `RECIPE_GROUPS`, `RECIPE_GROUP_ITEMS`, `TILE_COUNTS_AS` `:33-36`
- `availableRecipes(inv, ctx)` `:220-229` — environment × materials filtered list (what UI uses)
- `envOk` `:174`, `craftableCount` `:140`, `collectOwnedItems` `:95`, `consumeMaterials` `:232`
- `vanillaItemKey(vid)` `:202`, `ingredientKey(vid)` `:207` (resolves group ids → a display key)
- `invIndexOfVanilla` `:284`, `vanillaIdOfItemKey` `:89`, `expandStationTiles` `:76`
- `MATERIAL_VIDS` `:300-304` — **existing "is this item a crafting material" set**

**Reverse lookups — the gap and the existing piece:**
- ✅ **"Recipes consuming X" already implemented** in `game/src/ui/UI.ts:1499-1532` `renderGuideRecipes(listEl, vid)`: linear scan of `VANILLA_RECIPES` with `r.items.some(req => req.id === vid || (req.id >= 1000000 && RECIPE_GROUP_ITEMS[String(req.id-1000000)].includes(vid)))`, rendered as `sw-recipe` rows showing product icon/name/stack, station name (`Lang.tileName(r.tile)`), and full ingredient list. It is wired to a working panel — see §7.
- ⚠️ `getDecraftingRecipeIndex(vid, crimsonWorld)` `vanillaRecipes.ts:58-69` is the **only** create→recipe index, but it is **last-wins single entry** (built at `:43-54`), designed for shimmer-decraft, not for listing.
- ❌ **No `recipesProducing(vid)` helper exists** — nothing returns all recipes with `r.create === X`. Trivial to add (linear scan or build a `Map<number, number[]>` once, mirroring the `IS_CRAFTED` block at `:46-54`).

Game-level wrappers: `Game.vanillaAvailableRecipes()` `core/Game.ts:12580`, `Game.vanillaCraft()` `:12590`, `Game.stationsNearby()` `:12513`, shop stock table `Game.SHOPSTOCK` `:10119-10133`.

Consumed at `Bestiary.ts:319-320` and `BestiaryPanel.ts:361-371`, rendered as tags with localized labels. **This is the per-NPC spawn-condition table you asked about** — but it is bestiary-flavor text, not mechanical spawn rules.

- `.sw-root` fixed pointer-events:none container `UI.ts:146`; `.sw-panel` `:148-153` (vanilla UIPanel look); `.sw-slot` `:159-185`; `.sw-btn` `:154`; `.sw-tooltip` `:237`; `.sw-recipe` `:241-245` (**this is the recipe-row class you'd reuse for a wiki**)
- vui/Canvas is used **only for menu/worldgen states** — explicit at `mainFlow.ts:426-428`: "进游戏前清空 VUI（游戏内 UI 走 DOM，生成页仍用 VUI）". vui consumers: `VuiDemoState`, `GenWorldPreview`, `UIWorldLoadState`. BestiaryPanel/Settings/NpcDialog/UI import only `UISfx`/`UIScale` helpers from vui, not its widget tree.

**Input handling:** `Game.input.uiBlocking` (`core/Input.ts`) — set by `UI.openInventory` `UI.ts:1183`, cleared `:1201`; set+restored by `openBestiary` `mainFlow.ts:634-644`. Global click guard respects `.sw-panel/.sw-slot/.sw-btn` at `UI.ts:878, :1031, :1044`.

**Search box precedents (3, all DOM):**
1. **Crafting search box exists** — `UI.ts:1855-1859`, placeholder '搜索配方成品…', filters `availableRecipes` by product name at `:2353-2361`
2. Item search overlay `openItemSearch` `UI.ts:1208-1279`
3. Bestiary search `BestiaryPanel.ts:583, :611`

**Overlay+panel precedent:** `openItemSearch` `:1211-1236` and `openGuideSearch` `:1437-1464` both use `position:fixed; inset:0; background:rgba(0,0,0,0.55); z-index:1200` + centered panel + click-outside-to-close. `AchievementsUI.ts:95-192` is a module-level singleton-panel pattern (`openPanel`/`openEscHandler`/`achievementsPanelOpen()`).

---

## 6. DebugSummonPanel — `game/src/core/DebugSummonPanel.ts` (189 lines)

Self-contained DOM precedent, exactly the search+list pattern:
- Comment `:1-5`: "DOM 浮层（不进 vui 管线——纯开发工具，关掉即卸）", F6/Esc, list source = full NPC SetDefaults
- `SummonEntry` `:10-17` (id/name/boss/critter/town/lifeMax); `SUMMON_ENTRIES` `:20-35` built once at module level from `VANILLA_NPCS` + `TOWN_NPC_IDS` + `VANILLA_BOSS_IDS`
- **Pure filter function `filterSummonEntries(q, limit=240)` `:38-49`** — id/name substring match, exact-id-first ordering; exported for unit tests
- `STYLE` const `:65-71` (fixed top-right 350px, `z-index:99999`, dark translucent)
- `show()` `:87-131`: builds innerHTML template with `data-role` anchors, binds input/select/checkbox, focuses search; `render()` `:164-188` re-renders rows with inline styles + hover class toggle + per-category color coding; `msg()` status line `:159`; `close()` `:153-157` removes element
- `DebugSummonPanelHooks` `:51-63` — dependency-injected `spawn`/`clearEnemies`/`nameOf`/`events`/`triggerEvent`, keeping the panel pure

---

## 7. Existing "guide" strings / help panel

**There is no help panel**, but there is an existing guide-recipe feature and rich l10n:

- **`openGuideSearch()` `UI.ts:1435-1497`** — title `'📖 ' + Lang.text('LegacyInterface.21')` where `zh-Hans.json` `LegacyInterface.21` = **"所显示的配方使用此物品:"**; placeholder '材料名称 / key / 原版 id…'; item chip row + recipe list. Comment `:1430`: "向导配方反查（guideItem → CollectGuideRecipes，组匹配生效）". Opened by inventory button at `UI.ts:1802-1807`.
- **`openItemSearch()`** `UI.ts:1208` — 🔍 道具搜索 (debug item grant).
- l10n: `Controls` category has only 4 keys (LeftClick/RightClick/Shift/Ctrl). `GuideHelpText`/`GuideHelpTextSpecific`/`GuideChatter` are the vanilla Guide NPC's dialog, not a help system. `RecipeGroup` (4 keys).
- Bestiary text assets ready for reuse: `BestiaryInfo`, `Bestiary_ItemDropConditions` (42), `Bestiary_FlavorText` (564), `Bestiary_Biomes` (42), `Bestiary_BiomeText`, `Bestiary_Times`, `Bestiary_Events`, `Bestiary_Invasions`, `CommonBestiaryFlavor`.
- `Controls`/`Wiki`: `TitleLinks.Wiki` exists (menu link only).

**Offline corpus on disk (dev-time, not shipped to `game/public`):**
- `~/Project/GLM/SandboxWorld/terraria.wiki.gg_en_all_2026-07a.zim` (1.2GB)
- `~/Project/GLM/SandboxWorld/terraria-wiki-md/` — **655MB, ~9000 markdown pages** converted via `tools/zim-convert.py`
- `tools/wiki-mirror.mjs` mirrors the Chinese wiki (`terraria.wiki.gg/zh`) into `terraria-wiki-zh/` — that directory is currently absent/not run
- Extraction toolchain in `~/Project/GLM/SandboxWorld/tools/` (`extract-recipes.mjs`, `extract-npcdrops.mjs`, `extract-bestiary*.mjs`, `extract-itemfunc.mjs`, `extract-fishing.mjs`, `extract-shops.mjs`, `extract-bossbags.mjs`, `extract-shimmer.mjs`, `extract-npcs.mjs`, `build-id-maps.mjs`, `build-asset-table.mjs`, …) — all the `vanilla-*.json` files are generated by these.

---

## Reusable assets vs gaps

**Directly reusable (no new data needed):**
| Need | Asset |
|---|---|
| "某怪掉什么" | `BestiaryPanel.flattenDropsForNpc` `BestiaryPanel.ts:312` over `vanilla-npcdrops.json` |
| "某怪在哪出没" | `BestiaryPanel.bestiarySpawnConditions` `:377` over `vanilla-bestiary-spawn.json` (`byNpc`, 681) |
| "某物品能合成什么" (consuming X) | `UI.renderGuideRecipes` `UI.ts:1500` — **already shipped as a panel** |
| "某物品怎么合成" (producing X) | scan `VANILLA_RECIPES` by `create` — data complete, **helper missing** |
| Item/NPC/tile names (12 langs) | `idNames.generated.ts` + `Lang.ts` accessors |
| Item stats / function / rarity / value / use sound | `vanillaItemFunc.ts`, `vanillaItemStats.ts`, `vanilla-itemrare.json`, `vanilla-itemvalue.json`, `itemUseSound.ts` |
| NPC stats | `vanillaNpcs.ts` `VANILLA_NPCS` |
| Which NPC sells X | `vanilla-shopstock.json` `shops` (23 NPCs) + `Game.SHOPSTOCK` |
| Fish/angler acquisition | `vanillaFishing.ts` + `vanilla-fishing.json` |
| Shimmer conversions / decraft | `vanilla-shimmer.json` + `getDecraftingRecipeIndex` |
| Search + list DOM pattern | `DebugSummonPanel.ts`, `openItemSearch`/`openGuideSearch`/`openAchievementsPanel` |
| Recipe row / tooltip / slot CSS | `UI.ts:241-245`, `:237`, `:159` |

**Gaps:**
1. **No `recipesProducing(vid)` helper** — only the last-wins `getDecraftingRecipeIndex` `vanillaRecipes.ts:58`.
2. **No "which NPC drops item X" reverse index** — `dropRulesByNpc` is private in `BestiaryPanel.ts:236`; needs export or a shared walker that inverts to `Map<itemId, Array<{npcId, chance, cond}>>`.
3. **Item descriptions exist but are not consumed** — `ItemTooltip` (2664 zh keys), `CommonItemTooltip`, `LegacyTooltip` are unused by `UI.showTooltipFor` `UI.ts:1116`. A wiki can read them via `ITEM_NAME_BY_ID[vid]` → `Lang.text('ItemTooltip.' + name)`.
4. **No per-NPC mechanical spawn-condition table** — `vanilla-bestiary-spawn.json` is display-grade labels; `VanillaSpawner.ts` is code-only; `VANILLA_SPAWN_POOLS` `vanillaNpcs.ts:200` is a coarse pre-hardmode approximation (explicitly labeled as such).
5. **No acquisition-source aggregator** — recipes, drops, shops, fishing, boss bags, shimmer all exist separately by item id, but nothing joins them into a "how to obtain X" list.
6. **Bestiary data is unlock-gated** in the panel (`UnlockState` `Bestiary.ts:87`); a wiki would want the same data ungated.
7. **No in-game help panel** — `Controls` l10n is nearly empty; the only guide surface is the LegacyInterface.21 reverse-recipe panel.

No files were modified.
````
